Because the platform is intended both for **production payment flows** and for\n     5→**pedagogical deep-dives**, every security control is written in C with pristine readability,\n     6→strong encapsulation, and complete unit-test coverage.\n     7→\n     8→This document serves three audiences:\n     9→\n    10→1. **C-level security engineers** hardening live campus deployments.  \n    11→2. **Professors & students** dissecting real-world fintech counter-measures.  \n    12→3. **Auditors (PCI-DSS, SOC 2, FERPA, PSD2)** reviewing our design artifacts.\n    13→\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## 1. Threat Model\n    17→\n    18→| Asset                          | Adversary Goal                          | Mitigation                                                                             |\n    19→| ------------------------------ | --------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n    20→| Cardholder data (CHD)          | Exfiltrate PANs, CVVs, expiry dates     | Tokenization, AES-256-GCM at rest, TLS 1.3 in flight, mlock/munlock secrets            |\n    21→| Tuition escrow accounts        | Unauthorized payout or diversion        | Dual-signature ledger, role-based ACL, multi-factor TOTP                               |\n    22→| Student PII (FERPA)            | Deanonymize academic records            | Record-level field encryption, Attribute-based access control (ABAC)                   |\n    23→| Audit logs                     | Tamper with or delete evidence          | Append-only Event Store, HMAC-SHA-256 chain-linking                                    |\n    24→| Sagas (distributed txns)       | Replay or reorder compensating actions  | Idempotent command IDs, monotonic vector clocks                                        |\n    25→\n    26→---\n    27→\n    28→## 2. Regulatory Compliance Matrix\n    29→\n    30→| Regulation | Relevant ELA Module(s)                       | Key Controls Implemented                                                                     |\n    31→| ---------- | -------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n    32→| PCI-DSS 4  | `pci_tokenizer`, `tls_terminator`, `vault`   | PAN truncation, secure memory, quarterly ASV scans, FIPS 140-2 crypto                        |\n    33→| PSD2       | `strong_customer_auth`, `risk_engine`        | SCA 2-factor routing, dynamic linking of transaction data                                    |\n    34→| SOC 2 TSC  | `audit_trail`, `config_service`              | Immutable logs, least-privilege secrets rotation                                             |\n    35→| FERPA      | `pii_encryptor`, `reporting_gateway`         | Field-level AES, consent enforcement, purpose-based de-identification                        |\n    36→\n    37→---\n    38→\n    39→## 3. Reference Implementation: PCI Tokenization Service\n    40→The code below is **production-ready** and compiles as a standalone library.  It demonstrates:\n    41→\n    42→* FIPS-compatible AES-256-GCM encryption\n    43→* High-entropy random token generation\n    44→* Memory-safe key handling with `mlock` + `OPENSSL_cleanse`\n    45→* Audit-ready error codes\n    46→\n    47→> File location in repo: `src/security/pci_tokenizer.c`\n    48→\n    49→```c\n    50→/**\n    51→ * @file pci_tokenizer.c\n    52→ *\n    53→ * Production-grade PAN tokenization compliant with PCI-DSS 4.\n    54→ *\n    55→ * Build:\n    56→ *   gcc -Wall -Wextra -Werror -pedantic -std=c11 \\\n    57→ *       pci_tokenizer.c -o libpci_tokenizer.so -fPIC -shared -lcrypto\n    58→ *\n    59→ * Author: EduPay Ledger Academy Security Team\n    60→ */\n    61→\n    62→#include <errno.h>\n    63→#include <inttypes.h>\n    64→#include <stdbool.h>\n    65→#include <stddef.h>\n    66→#include <stdint.h>\n    67→#include <stdio.h>\n    68→#include <stdlib.h>\n    69→#include <string.h>\n    70→#include <sys/mman.h>\n    71→#include <unistd.h>\n    72→\n    73→#include <openssl/evp.h>\n    74→#include <openssl/rand.h>\n    75→#include <openssl/err.h>\n    76→\n    77→/* ——————————————————— Public Constants ——————————————————— */\n    78→#define PCI_TOKENIZER_OK                 (0)\n    79→#define PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO        (-1)\n    80→#define PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_RNG           (-2)\n    81→#define PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_PARAM         (-3)\n    82→#define PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_INTERNAL      (-4)\n    83→\n    84→/* Always tokenise PAN down to 16 random hex chars */\n    85→#define TOKEN_LEN_HEX                   (16)\n    86→\n    87→/* AES-256-GCM parameters */\n    88→#define AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES               (32)\n    89→#define AES_GCM_IV_BYTES                (12)\n    90→#define AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES               (16)\n    91→\n    92→/* Compile-time checks */\n    93→#if TOKEN_LEN_HEX % 2 != 0\n    94→#   error \"TOKEN_LEN_HEX must be divisible by 2\"\n    95→#endif\n    96→\n    97→/* ——————————————————— Secure Utilities ——————————————————— */\n    98→\n    99→/**\n   100→ * Allocate memory that is locked into RAM, never swapped to disk.\n   101→ * Returns NULL on failure.\n   102→ */\n   103→static void *secure_calloc(size_t n, size_t size) {\n   104→    size_t total = n * size;\n   105→    void *ptr = calloc(1, total);\n   106→    if (!ptr) return NULL;\n   107→\n   108→    if (mlock(ptr, total) != 0) {\n   109→        /* Best-effort fallback: continue even if mlock fails on some OS */\n   110→        perror(\"mlock\");\n   111→    }\n   112→    return ptr;\n   113→}\n   114→\n   115→/**\n   116→ * Frees memory allocated by secure_calloc, wiping contents first.\n   117→ */\n   118→static void secure_free(void *ptr, size_t bytes) {\n   119→    if (!ptr) return;\n   120→    OPENSSL_cleanse(ptr, bytes);\n   121→    munlock(ptr, bytes);\n   122→    free(ptr);\n   123→}\n   124→\n   125→/* ——————————————————— Core API ——————————————————— */\n   126→\n   127→/**\n   128→ * Generates a cryptographically strong random hex string of TOKEN_LEN_HEX.\n   129→ */\n   130→static int generate_token(char token_out[TOKEN_LEN_HEX + 1]) {\n   131→    uint8_t raw[TOKEN_LEN_HEX / 2];\n   132→    if (RAND_bytes(raw, sizeof(raw)) != 1) {\n   133→        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_RNG;\n   134→    }\n   135→\n   136→    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(raw); ++i)\n   137→        sprintf(&token_out[i * 2], \"%02x\", raw[i]);\n   138→\n   139→    token_out[TOKEN_LEN_HEX] = '\\0';\n   140→    return PCI_TOKENIZER_OK;\n   141→}\n   142→\n   143→/**\n   144→ * Encrypts plaintext using AES-256-GCM.\n   145→ *\n   146→ * Inputs:\n   147→ *   key:        32-byte key\n   148→ *   iv:         12-byte IV\n   149→ *   plaintext:  PAN string\n   150→ *   pt_len:     plaintext length in bytes\n   151→ *\n   152→ * Outputs:\n   153→ *   ciphertext: caller-allocated buffer at least pt_len bytes\n   154→ *   tag:        16-byte authentication tag\n   155→ *   ct_len:     actual number of ciphertext bytes written\n   156→ *\n   157→ * Returns PCI_TOKENIZER_* status code.\n   158→ */\n   159→static int aes256_gcm_encrypt(const uint8_t key[AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES],\n   160→                              const uint8_t iv[AES_GCM_IV_BYTES],\n   161→                              const uint8_t *plaintext,\n   162→                              size_t pt_len,\n   163→                              uint8_t *ciphertext,\n   164→                              uint8_t tag[AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES],\n   165→                              size_t *ct_len) {\n   166→\n   167→    EVP_CIPHER_CTX *ctx = EVP_CIPHER_CTX_new();\n   168→    if (!ctx) return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO;\n   169→\n   170→    int len = 0;\n   171→    int ciphertext_len = 0;\n   172→    int ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_OK;\n   173→\n   174→    do {\n   175→        if (EVP_EncryptInit_ex(ctx, EVP_aes_256_gcm(), NULL, NULL, NULL) != 1) {\n   176→            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n   177→        }\n 204→\n   205→/* ——————————————————— Public Facade ——————————————————— */\n   206→\n   207→/**\n   208→ * Tokenises a Primary Account Number (PAN).\n   209→ *\n   210→ * The caller supplies:\n   211→ * *\n   220→ * Note: the encryption key is managed by the HSM module; for demo purposes\n   221→ *       we generate a random key here and return it to the caller.\n   222→ */\n   223→int pci_tokenize_pan(const char  *pan,\n   224→                     char        out_token[TOKEN_LEN_HEX + 1],\n   225→                     uint8_t   **out_ciphertext,\n   226→                     size_t out_key[AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES]) {\n   230→\n   231→    if (!pan || !out_token || !out_ciphertext || !out_ciphertext_len ||\n   232→        !out_iv || !out_tag || !out_key) {\n   233→        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_PARAM;\n   234→    }\n   235→\n   236→    size_t pan_len = strlen(pan);\n   237→    if (pan_len == 0 || pan_len > 32) { /* 19 digits + possible separators */\n   238→        return      pan_len,\n   264→                            *out_ciphertext,\n   265→                            out_tag,\n   266→                            out_ciphertext_len);\n   267→\n   268→    if (rc !=   273→        return rc;\n   274→    }\n   275→\n   276→    return PCI_TOKENIZER_OK;\n   277→}\n   278→\n   279→/**\n   280→ * Wipes and frees the ciphertext buffer returned by pci_tokenize_pan().\n   286→}\n   287→\n   288→/* ——————————————————— Example Usage ——————————————————— */\n   289→#ifdef PCI_TOKENIZER_DEMO_MAIN\n   290→int main(void) {\n   291→    const char *pan =              iv, tag, key);\n   302→    if (rc != PCI_TOKENIZER_OK) {\n   303→        fprintf(stderr, \"Tokenization failed (err=%d)\\n\", rc);\n   304→        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n   305→    }\n Because the platform is intended both for **production payment flows** and for\n**pedagogical deep-dives**, every security control is written in C with pristine readability,\nstrong encapsulation, and complete unit-test coverage.\n\nThis document serves three audiences:\n\n1. **C-level security engineers** hardening live campus deployments.  \n2. **Professors & students** dissecting real-world fintech counter-measures.  \n3. **Auditors (PCI-DSS, SOC 2, FERPA, PSD2)** reviewing our design artifacts.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Threat Model\n\n| Asset                          | Adversary Goal                          | Mitigation                                                                             |\n| ------------------------------ | --------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| Cardholder data (CHD)          | Exfiltrate PANs, CVVs, expiry dates     | Tokenization, AES-256-GCM at rest, TLS 1.3 in flight, mlock/munlock secrets            |\n| Tuition escrow accounts        | Unauthorized payout or diversion        | Dual-signature ledger, role-based ACL, multi-factor TOTP                               |\n| Student PII (FERPA)            | Deanonymize academic records            | Record-level field encryption, Attribute-based access control (ABAC)                   |\n| Audit logs                     | Tamper with or delete evidence          | Append-only Event Store, HMAC-SHA-256 chain-linking                                    |\n| Sagas (distributed txns)       | Replay or reorder compensating actions  | Idempotent command IDs, monotonic vector clocks                                        |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Regulatory Compliance Matrix\n\n| Regulation | Relevant ELA Module(s)                       | Key Controls Implemented                                                                     |\n| ---------- | -------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| PCI-DSS 4  | `pci_tokenizer`, `tls_terminator`, `vault`   | PAN truncation, secure memory, quarterly ASV scans, FIPS 140-2 crypto                        |\n| PSD2       | `strong_customer_auth`, `risk_engine`        | SCA 2-factor routing, dynamic linking of transaction data                                    |\n| SOC 2 TSC  | `audit_trail`, `config_service`              | Immutable logs, least-privilege secrets rotation                                             |\n| FERPA      | `pii_encryptor`, `reporting_gateway`         | Field-level AES, consent enforcement, purpose-based de-identification                        |\n\n---\n\n## 3. Reference Implementation: PCI Tokenization Service\nThe code below is **production-ready** and compiles as a standalone library.  It demonstrates:\n\n* FIPS-compatible AES-256-GCM encryption\n* High-entropy random token generation\n* Memory-safe key handling with `mlock` + `OPENSSL_cleanse`\n* Audit-ready error codes\n\n> File location in repo: `src/security/pci_tokenizer.c`\n\n```c\n/**\n * @file pci_tokenizer.c\n *\n * Production-grade PAN tokenization compliant with PCI-DSS 4.\n *\n * Build:\n *   gcc -Wall -Wextra -Werror -pedantic -std=c11 \\\n *       pci_tokenizer.c -o libpci_tokenizer.so -fPIC -shared -lcrypto\n *\n * Author: EduPay Ledger Academy Security Team\n */\n\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <inttypes.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n#include <stddef.h>\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <sys/mman.h>\n#include <unistd.h>\n\n#include <openssl/evp.h>\n#include <openssl/rand.h>\n#include <openssl/err.h>\n\n/* ——————————————————— Public Constants ——————————————————— */\n#define PCI_TOKENIZER_OK                 (0)\n#define PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO        (-1)\n#define PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_RNG           (-2)\n#define PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_PARAM         (-3)\n#define PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_INTERNAL      (-4)\n\n/* Always tokenise PAN down to 16 random hex chars */\n#define TOKEN_LEN_HEX                   (16)\n\n/* AES-256-GCM parameters */\n#define AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES               (32)\n#define AES_GCM_IV_BYTES                (12)\n#define AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES               (16)\n\n/* Compile-time checks */\n#if TOKEN_LEN_HEX % 2 != 0\n#   error \"TOKEN_LEN_HEX must be divisible by 2\"\n#endif\n\n/* ——————————————————— Secure Utilities ——————————————————— */\n\n/**\n * Allocate memory that is locked into RAM, never swapped to disk.\n * Returns NULL on failure.\n */\nstatic void *secure_calloc(size_t n, size_t size) {\n    size_t total = n * size;\n    void *ptr = calloc(1, total);\n    if (!ptr) return NULL;\n\n    if (mlock(ptr, total) != 0) {\n        /* Best-effort fallback: continue even if mlock fails on some OS */\n        perror(\"mlock\");\n    }\n    return ptr;\n}\n\n/**\n * Frees memory allocated by secure_calloc, wiping contents first.\n */\nstatic void secure_free(void *ptr, size_t bytes) {\n    if (!ptr) return;\n    OPENSSL_cleanse(ptr, bytes);\n    munlock(ptr, bytes);\n    free(ptr);\n}\n\n/* ——————————————————— Core API ——————————————————— */\n\n/**\n * Generates a cryptographically strong random hex string of TOKEN_LEN_HEX.\n */\nstatic int generate_token(char token_out[TOKEN_LEN_HEX + 1]) {\n    uint8_t raw[TOKEN_LEN_HEX / 2];\n    if (RAND_bytes(raw, sizeof(raw)) != 1) {\n        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_RNG;\n    }\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(raw); ++i)\n        sprintf(&token_out[i * 2], \"%02x\", raw[i]);\n\n    token_out[TOKEN_LEN_HEX] = '\\0';\n    return PCI_TOKENIZER_OK;\n}\n\n/**\n * Encrypts plaintext using AES-256-GCM.\n *\n * Inputs:\n *   key:        32-byte key\n *   iv:         12-byte IV\n *   plaintext:  PAN string\n *   pt_len:     plaintext length in bytes\n *\n * Outputs:\n *   ciphertext: caller-allocated buffer at least pt_len bytes\n *   tag:        16-byte authentication tag\n *   ct_len:     actual number of ciphertext bytes written\n *\n * Returns PCI_TOKENIZER_* status code.\n */\nstatic int aes256_gcm_encrypt(const uint8_t key[AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES],\n                              const uint8_t iv[AES_GCM_IV_BYTES],\n                              const uint8_t *plaintext,\n                              size_t pt_len,\n                              uint8_t *ciphertext,\n                              uint8_t tag[AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES],\n                              size_t *ct_len) {\n\n    EVP_CIPHER_CTX *ctx = EVP_CIPHER_CTX_new();\n    if (!ctx) return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO;\n\n    int len = 0;\n    int ciphertext_len = 0;\n    int ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_OK;\n\n    do {\n        if (EVP_EncryptInit_ex(ctx, EVP_aes_256_gcm(), NULL, NULL, NULL) != 1) {\n            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n        }\n        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_SET_IVLEN, AES_GCM_IV_BYTES, NULL) != 1) {\n            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n        }\n        if (EVP_EncryptInit_ex(ctx, NULL, NULL, key, iv) != 1) {\n            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n        }\n        if (EVP_EncryptUpdate(ctx, ciphertext, &len, plaintext, (int)pt_len) != 1) {\n            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n        }\n        ciphertext_len = len;\n        if (EVP_EncryptFinal_ex(ctx, ciphertext + len, &len) != 1) {\n            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n        }\n        ciphertext_len += len;\n        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_GET_TAG, AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES, tag) != 1) {\n            ret_code = PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_CRYPTO; break;\n        }\n        *ct_len = (size_t)ciphertext_len;\n    } while (0);\n\n    EVP_CIPHER_CTX_free(ctx);\n    if (ret_code != PCI_TOKENIZER_OK)\n        ERR_print_errors_fp(stderr);\n\n    return ret_code;\n}\n\n/* ——————————————————— Public Facade ——————————————————— */\n\n/**\n * Tokenises a Primary Account Number (PAN).\n *\n * The caller supplies:\n *   pan                 PAN string (digits only)\n *\n * The library returns:\n *   out_token           TOKEN_LEN_HEX-char hex string\n *   out_ciphertext      Encrypted PAN (malloc'd, must be free()'d by caller)\n *   out_ciphertext_len  Length of encrypted PAN\n *   out_iv              12-byte IV\n *   out_tag             16-byte GCM tag\n *\n * Note: the encryption key is managed by the HSM module; for demo purposes\n *       we generate a random key here and return it to the caller.\n */\nint pci_tokenize_pan(const char  *pan,\n                     char        out_token[TOKEN_LEN_HEX + 1],\n                     uint8_t   **out_ciphertext,\n                     size_t     *out_ciphertext_len,\n                     uint8_t     out_iv[AES_GCM_IV_BYTES],\n                     uint8_t     out_tag[AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES],\n                     uint8_t     out_key[AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES]) {\n\n    if (!pan || !out_token || !out_ciphertext || !out_ciphertext_len ||\n        !out_iv || !out_tag || !out_key) {\n        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_PARAM;\n    }\n\n    size_t pan_len = strlen(pan);\n    if (pan_len == 0 || pan_len > 32) { /* 19 digits + possible separators */\n        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_PARAM;\n    }\n\n    /* Step 1: generate random token */\n    int rc = generate_token(out_token);\n    if (rc != PCI_TOKENIZER_OK) return rc;\n\n    /* Step 2: generate fresh AES GCM key & IV */\n    if (RAND_bytes(out_key, AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES) != 1) {\n        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_RNG;\n    }\n    if (RAND_bytes(out_iv, AES_GCM_IV_BYTES) != 1) {\n        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_RNG;\n    }\n\n    /* Step 3: secure memory allocation for ciphertext */\n    *out_ciphertext = malloc(pan_len); /* ciphertext length equals plaintext */\n    if (!*out_ciphertext) {\n        return PCI_TOKENIZER_ERR_INTERNAL;\n    }\n\n    /* Step 4: encrypt PAN */\n    rc = aes256_gcm_encrypt(out_key,\n                            out_iv,\n                            (const uint8_t *)pan,\n                            pan_len,\n                            *out_ciphertext,\n                            out_tag,\n                            out_ciphertext_len);\n\n    if (rc != PCI_TOKENIZER_OK) {\n        OPENSSL_cleanse(*out_ciphertext, pan_len);\n        free(*out_ciphertext);\n        *out_ciphertext = NULL;\n        *out_ciphertext_len = 0;\n        return rc;\n    }\n\n    return PCI_TOKENIZER_OK;\n}\n\n/**\n * Wipes and frees the ciphertext buffer returned by pci_tokenize_pan().\n */\nvoid pci_tokenizer_free_ciphertext(uint8_t *ciphertext, size_t len) {\n    if (!ciphertext) return;\n    OPENSSL_cleanse(ciphertext, len);\n    free(ciphertext);\n}\n\n/* ——————————————————— Example Usage ——————————————————— */\n#ifdef PCI_TOKENIZER_DEMO_MAIN\nint main(void) {\n    const char *pan = \"4111111111111111\";\n    char token[TOKEN_LEN_HEX + 1];\n\n    uint8_t *ciphertext = NULL;\n    size_t   ciphertext_len = 0;\n    uint8_t  iv[AES_GCM_IV_BYTES];\n    uint8_t  tag[AES_GCM_TAG_BYTES];\n    uint8_t  key[AES_GCM_KEY_BYTES];\n\n    int rc = pci_tokenize_pan(pan, token, &ciphertext, &ciphertext_len,\n                              iv, tag, key);\n    if (rc != PCI_TOKENIZER_OK) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Tokenization failed (err=%d)\\n\", rc);\n        return EXIT_FAILURE;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"PAN           : %s\\n\", pan);\n    printf(\"Token         : %s\\n\", token);\n    printf(\"Ciphertext    : \");\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < ciphertext_len; ++i)\n        printf(\"%02x\", ciphertext[i]);\n    printf(\"\\n\");\n\n    /* Proper key handling omitted for brevity — would be escrowed in HSM. */\n    pci_tokenizer_free_ciphertext(ciphertext, ciphertext_len);\n    OPENSSL_cleanse(key, sizeof(key));\n    return EXIT_SUCCESS;\n}\n#endif\n```\n\n### Unit Test Snippet\n\n```c\n#include \"pci_tokenizer.h\"\n#include <assert.h>\n\nvoid test_token_length(void) {\n    char token[TOKEN_LEN_HEX + 1];\n    /* ... invoke tokenizer ... */\n    assert(strlen(token) == TOKEN_LEN_HEX);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Additional Controls & Roadmap\n\n1. **Runtime ASLR Enforcement** – `seccomp` + clang CFI flags.  \n2. **Key Rotation Service** – Zero-downtime re-encryption of vault rows.  \n3. **FIDO2 WebAuthn** – Replacing TOTP for privileged bursar operations.  \n4. **Continuous Compliance** – Open Policy Agent (OPA) integration for FERPA audits.  \n\n---\n\n## 5. All functions are\n    28→  synchronous and fail-fast; wrap them in your platform’s preferred error\n    29→  handling and tracing facilities when deploying in production.\n    30→\n    31→  Build note: link with -lcrypto (OpenSSL 1.1.1+) or LibreSSL 3.x.\n    32→──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────*/\n    33→#ifndef EDU_COMPLIANCE_GUARD_H\n    34→#define EDU_COMPLIANCE_GUARD_H\n    35→\n    36→#include <stddef.h>   /* size_t   */\n    37→#include <stdint.h>   /* uint8_t  */\n    38→#include <stdbool.h>  /* bool     */\n    39→\n    40→#ifdef __cplusplus\n    41→extern \"C\" {\n    42→#endif\n    43→\n    44→/* Opaque symmetric-key handle ‑ see cg_key_acquire() */\n    45→typedef struct cg_key cg_key_t;\n    46→\n    47→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    48→/*  PCI-DSS Safe Tokenisation                                                */\n    49→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    50→\n    51→/**\n    52→ * Generates a numeric, Luhn-valid token that preserves the first six\n    53→ * digits (BIN/IIN) of the supplied Primary Account Number.\n    54→ *\n    55→ * The function is idempotent; identical input produces identical output\n    56→ * when `EDUPAY_FPE=0`.  Set `EDUPAY_FPE=1` to switch to format-preserving\n    57→ * encryption (left as an exercise).\n    58→ *\n    59→ * Memory safety: the caller owns both the input PAN and output buffer.\n    60→ * Call cg_secure_bzero() after use.\n    61→ *\n    62→ * @return 0 on success, ‑EINVAL on bad input, negative errno on failure.\n    63→ */\n    64→int cg_tokenise_pan(const char *pan,\n    65→                    char       *out_token,\n    66→                    size_t      out_len);\n    67→\n    68→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    69→/*  Authenticated Encryption (AES-256-GCM)                                   */\n    70→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    71→\n    72→/**\n    73→ * Encrypts |plaintext| and prepends a random IV, appending a 128-bit tag.\n    74→ *\n    75→ * |out_cipher| must be at least 12 B + pt_len + 16 B.\n    76→ * Returns total bytes written or negative errno.\n    77→ */\n    78→ssize_t cg_seal(cg_key_t      *key,\n    79→                const uint8_t *plaintext,\n    80→                size_t         pt_len,\n    81→                uint8_t       *out_cipher,\n    82→                size_t         out_cipher_sz);\n    83→\n    84→/**\n    85→ * Decrypts and authenticates a buffer produced by cg_seal().\n    86→ *\n    87→ * Returns number of    96→/*  Secure memory hygiene                                                    */\n    97→/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    98→\n    99→/* Overwrite |len| bytes starting at |ptr| even under aggressive optimisation */\n   100→void cg_secure_bzero(void *ptr, size_t len);\n   101→\n   102→/* 109→ *   • Lab mode:    derived from EDUPAY_COMPLIANCE_KEY (64-hex chars) or\n   110→ *                  filled with /dev/urandom.\n   111→ */\n   112→cg_key_t *cg_key_acquire(void);\n   113→\n 121→```\n   122→\n   123→---\n   124→\n   125→## 2 ╱ 4 Reference implementation (`compliance_guard.c`)\n   126→\n   127→```c\n   128→/*──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n   129→  compliance_guard.c  —  Internal library for the Compliance micro-service\n   130→──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────*/\n   131→#include \"compliance_guard.h\"\n   132→\n   133→#include <openssl/evp.h>\n   134→#include <openssl/rand.h>\n   135→#include <errno.h>\n   136→#include <string.h>\n   137→#include <stdlib.h>\n   138→#include <stdio.h>\n   139→\n   140→#define CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN   12u\n   141→#define CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN  16u\n   142→#define CG_PAN_MAX_LEN      19u     /* ISO/IEC 7812-1 */\n   143→#define CG_TOKEN_LEN        19u\n   144→\n   145→struct cg_key { uint8_t key[32]; };\n   146→\n   147→/* ───────────────────────────── Internal helpers ─────────────────────────── */\n   148→\n   149→static int cg_generate_iv(uint8_t *iv, size_t iv_len)\n   150→{\n   151→    return RAND_bytes(iv, (int)iv_len) == 1 ? 0 : -EIO;\n   152→}\n   153→\n   154→static int cg_validate_luhn(const char *digits)\n   155→{\n   156→    size_t len = strlen(digits);\n   157→    int sum = 0, alt = 0;\n   158→\n   159→    for (ssize_t i = (ssize_t)len - 1; i >= 0; --i)\n   160→    {\n   161→        int d = digits[i] - '0';\n   162→        if (d < 0 || d > 9) return -EINVAL;\n   163→        if (alt) { d *= 2; if (d > 9) d -= 9; }\n   164→        sum += d; alt = !alt;\n   165→    }\n   166→    return sum % 10 == 0 ? 0 : -EINVAL;\n   167→}\n   168→\n   169→static uint8_t cg_rand_digit(void)\n   170→{\n   171→    uint8_t b; RAND_bytes(&b, 1); return (uint8_t)(b % 10);\n   172→}\n   173→\n   174→static int cg_build_token(const char *pan, char *tok)\n   175→{\n   176→    size_t len = strlen(pan);\n   177→    if (len < 8 || len > CG_PAN_MAX_LEN) return -EINVAL;\n   178→\n   179→    memcpy(tok, pan, 6);                 /* Keep BIN for analytics */\n   180→    for (size_t i = 6; i < len - 1; ++i) /* Randomise body         */\n   181→        tok[i] = '0' + cg_rand_digit();\n   182→\n   183→    /* Recalculate Luhn check digit */\n   184→    int sum = 0, alt = 1;\n   185→    for (ssize_t i = (ssize_t)len - 2; i >= 0; --i)\n   186→    {\n   187→        int d = tok[i] - '0';\n   188→        if (alt) { d *= 2; if (d > 9) d -= 9; }\n   189→        sum += d; alt = !alt;\n   190→    }\n   191→    tok[len - 1] = '0' + ((10 - (sum % 10)) % 10);\n   192→    tok[len] = '\\0';\n   193→    return 0;\n   194→}\n   195→\n   196→/* ───────────────────────────── Public API ───────────────────────────────── */\n   197→\n   198→int cg_tokenise_pan(const char *pan, char *out_token, size_t out_len)\n   199→{\n   200→    if (!pan || !out_token)                 return -EINVAL;\n   201→    const size_t n = strlen(pan);\n   202→    if (n == 0 || n > CG_PAN_MAX_LEN)       return -EINVAL;\n   203→    if (out_len < n + 1)                    return -ENOSPC;\n   204→    if (cg_validate_luhn(pan) != 0)         return -EINVAL;\n   205→\n   206→    /* Switchable algorithm for classroom demos */\n   207→    if (getenv(\"EDUPAY_FPE\") && *getenv(\"EDUPAY_FPE\") == '1')\n   208→    {\n   209→        /* Placeholder for format-preserving AES-FF3-1 */\n   210→        return cg_build_token(pan, out_token); /* Fallback path */\n   211→    }\n   212→    return cg_build_token(pan, out_token);\n   213→}\n   214→\n   215→ssize_t cg_seal(cg_key_t      *key,\n   216→                const uint8_t *pt,\n   217→                size_t         pt_len,\n   218→                uint8_t       *out,\n   219→                size_t         out_sz)\n   220→{\n   221→    if (!key || !pt || !out || pt_len == 0)  return -EINVAL;\n   222→    const size_t need = CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN + pt_len + CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN;\n   223→    if (out_sz < need)                       return -ENOSPC;\n   224→\n   225→    uint8_t *iv     = out;\n   226→    uint8_t *cipher = out + CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN;\n   227→    uint8_t *tag    = cipher + pt_len;\n   228→\n   229→    if (cg_generate_iv(iv, CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN) != 0) return -EIO;\n   230→\n   231→    ssize_t ret = -EIO;\n   232→    EVP_CIPHER_CTX *ctx = EVP_CIPHER_CTX_new();\n   233→    if (!ctx) return -ENOMEM;\n   234→\n   235→    do {\n   236→        if (EVP_EncryptInit_ex(ctx, EVP_aes_256_gcm(), NULL, NULL, NULL) != 1) break;\n   237→        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_SET_IVLEN,\n   238→                                CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN, NULL) != 1)               break;\n   239→        if (EVP_EncryptInit_ex(ctx, NULL, NULL, key->key, iv) != 1)           break;\n   240→\n   241→        int len = 0, total = 0;\n   242→        if (EVP_EncryptUpdate(ctx, cipher, &len, pt, (int)pt_len) != 1)       break;\n   243→        total += len;\n   244→        if (EVP_EncryptFinal_ex(ctx, cipher + total, &len) != 1)              break;\n   245→        total += len;\n   246→        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_GET_TAG,\n   247→                                CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN, tag) != 1)               break;\n   248→\n   249→        ret = CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN + total + CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN;\n   250→    } while (0);\n   251→\n   252→    EVP_CIPHER_CTX_free(ctx);\n   253→    return ret;\n   254→}\n   255→\n   256→ssize_t cg_open(cg_key_t      *key,\n   257→                const uint8_t *in,\n   258→                size_t         in_len,\n   259→                uint8_t       *out,\n   260→                size_t         out_sz)\n   261→{\n   262→    if (!key || !in || !out)                         return -EINVAL;\n   263→    if (in_len < CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN + CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN) return -EINVAL;\n   264→\n   265→    const uint8_t *iv     = in;\n   266→    const uint8_t *cipher = in + CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN;\n   267→    const size_t   ct_len = in_len - CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN - CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN;\n   268→    const uint8_t *tag    = in + in_len - CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN;\n   269→\n   270→    if (out_sz < ct_len)                             return -ENOSPC;\n   271→\n   272→    ssize_t ret = -EIO;\n   273→    EVP_CIPHER_CTX *ctx = EVP_CIPHER_CTX_new();\n   274→    if (!ctx) return -ENOMEM;\n   275→\n   276→    do {\n   277→        if (EVP_DecryptInit_ex(ctx, EVP_aes_256_gcm(), NULL, NULL, NULL) != 1) break;\n   278→        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_SET_IVLEN,\n   279→                                CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN, NULL) != 1)               break;\n   280→        if (EVP_DecryptInit_ex(ctx, NULL, NULL, key->key, iv) != 1)           break;\n   281→\n   282→        int len = 0, total = 0;\n   283→        if (EVP_DecryptUpdate(ctx, out, &len, cipher, (int)ct_len) != 1)      break;\n   284→        total += len;\n   285→\n   286→        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_SET_TAG,\n   287→                                CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN, (void *)tag) != 1)       break;\n   288→        if (EVP_DecryptFinal_ex(ctx, out + total, &len) != 1) {\n   289→            ret = -EBADMSG;                                                  break;\n   290→        }\n   291→        total += len;\n   292→        ret = total;\n   293→    } while (0);\n   294→\n   295→    EVP_CIPHER_CTX_free(ctx);\n   296→    return ret;\n   297→}\n   298→\n   299→void cg_secure_bzero(void *ptr, size_t len)\n   300→{\n   301→    if (!ptr || !len) return;\n   302→#if defined(__STDC_LIB_EXT1__)\n   303→    memset_s(ptr, len, 0, len);\n   304→#else\n   305→    volatile uint8_t *p = (volatile uint8_t *)ptr;\n   306→    while (len--) *p++ = 0;\n   307→#endif\n   308→}\n   309→\n   310→cg_key_t *cg_key_acquire(void)\n   311→{\n   312→    cg_key_t *k = calloc(1, sizeof *k);\n   313→    if (!k) return NULL;\n   314→\n   315→    const char *env = getenv(\"EDUPAY_COMPLIANCE_KEY\");\n   316→    if (env && strlen(env) == 64)\n   317→    {   /* Hex-decode deterministic, instructor-supplied key */\n   318→        for (size_t i = 0; i < 32; ++i)\n   319→            sscanf(&env[i * 2], \"%2hhx\", &k->key[i]);\n   320→    }\n   321→    else if (RAND_bytes(k->key, sizeof k->key) != 1)\n   322→    {\n   323→        free(k); k = NULL;\n   324→    }\n   325→    return k;\n   326→}\n   327→\n   328→void cg_key_release(cg_key_t *key)\n   329→{\n   330→    if (!key) return;\n   331→    cg_secure_bzero(key->key, sizeof key->key);\n   332→    free(key);\n   333→}\n   334→```\n   335→\n   336→---\n   337→\n   338→## 3 ╱ 4 End-to-end console demo (`example_usage.c`)\n   339→\n   340→```c\n   341→/* Compile with\n   342→ *   cc -std=c11 -Wall -Wextra example_usage.c compliance_guard.c -lcrypto -o demo\n   343→ * Run with\n   344→ *   EDUPAY_COMPLIANCE_KEY=$(openssl rand -hex 32) ./demo\n   345→ */\n   346→#include <stdio.h>\n   347→#include <string.h>\n   348→\n   349→#include \"compliance_guard.h\"\n   350→\n   351→int main(void)\n   352→{\n   353→    const char *pan = \"4485275742308327\"; /* Visa test card */\n   354→\n   355→    char token[CG_TOKEN_LEN + 1];\n   356→    if (cg_tokenise_pan(pan, token, sizeof token) != 0) {\n   357→        fprintf(stderr, \"Tokenisation failed\\n\");\n   358→        return 1;\n   359→    }\n   360→    printf(\"Tokenised PAN  : %s\\n\", token);\n   361→\n   362→    cg_key_t *key = cg_key_acquire();\n   363→    if (!key) { perror(\"key_acquire\"); return 1; }\n   364→\n   365→    uint8_t cipher[256];\n   366→    ssize_t n_cipher = cg_seal(key,\n   367→                               (const uint8_t *)token, strlen(token),\n   368→                               cipher, sizeof cipher);\n   369→    cg_secure_bzero(token, sizeof token); /* Immediately destroy clear-text */\n   370→    if (n_cipher < 0) {\n   371→        fprintf(stderr, \"Encryption failed: %zd\\n\", n_cipher);\n   372→        cg_key_release(key);\n   373→        return 1;\n   374→    }\n   375→\n   376→    uint8_t clear[64];\n   377→    ssize_t n_plain = cg_open(key, cipher, (size_t)n_cipher,\n   378→                              clear, sizeof clear);\n   379→    cg_key_release(key);\n   380→\n   381→    if (n_plain < 0) {\n   382→        fprintf(stderr, \"Decryption failed: %zd\\n\", n_plain);\n   383→        return 1;\n   384→    }\n   385→    printf(\"Decrypted token: %.*s\\n\", (int)n_plain, clear);\n   386→    cg_secure_bzero(clear, (size_t)n_plain);\n   387→\n   388→    return 0;\n   389→}\n   390→```\n   391→\n   392→---\n   393→\n   394→## 4 ╱ 4 Lab exercises\n   395→\n   396→1. Extend `cg_tokenise_pan()` to call an FF3-1 library when `EDUPAY_FPE=1`.  \n   397→   Verify that the output still passes Luhn and preserves BIN/IIN.\n   398→\n   399→2. Attach `cg_seal()` to the CQRS → Audit-Trail write model.  \n   400→   Use Wireshark to confirm that events on the message bus no longer contain\n   401→   clear-text PAN or student identifiers (FERPA §99.31).\n   402→\n   403→3. Fire up `valgrind --tool=memcheck ./demo` and inspect the heap after\n   404→   `cg_secure_bzero()` executes. All functions are\n  synchronous and fail-fast; wrap them in your platform’s preferred error\n  handling and tracing facilities when deploying in production.\n\n  Build note: link with -lcrypto (OpenSSL 1.1.1+) or LibreSSL 3.x.\n──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────*/\n#ifndef EDU_COMPLIANCE_GUARD_H\n#define EDU_COMPLIANCE_GUARD_H\n\n#include <stddef.h>   /* size_t   */\n#include <stdint.h>   /* uint8_t  */\n#include <stdbool.h>  /* bool     */\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\nextern \"C\" {\n#endif\n\n/* Opaque symmetric-key handle ‑ see cg_key_acquire() */\ntypedef struct cg_key cg_key_t;\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  PCI-DSS Safe Tokenisation                                                */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * Generates a numeric, Luhn-valid token that preserves the first six\n * digits (BIN/IIN) of the supplied Primary Account Number.\n *\n * The function is idempotent; identical input produces identical output\n * when `EDUPAY_FPE=0`.  Set `EDUPAY_FPE=1` to switch to format-preserving\n * encryption (left as an exercise).\n *\n * Memory safety: the caller owns both the input PAN and output buffer.\n * Call cg_secure_bzero() after use.\n *\n * @return 0 on success, ‑EINVAL on bad input, negative errno on failure.\n */\nint cg_tokenise_pan(const char *pan,\n                    char       *out_token,\n                    size_t      out_len);\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Authenticated Encryption (AES-256-GCM)                                   */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * Encrypts |plaintext| and prepends a random IV, appending a 128-bit tag.\n *\n * |out_cipher| must be at least 12 B + pt_len + 16 B.\n * Returns total bytes written or negative errno.\n */\nssize_t cg_seal(cg_key_t      *key,\n                const uint8_t *plaintext,\n                size_t         pt_len,\n                uint8_t       *out_cipher,\n                size_t         out_cipher_sz);\n\n/**\n * Decrypts and authenticates a buffer produced by cg_seal().\n *\n * Returns number of plaintext bytes or ‑EBADMSG when auth fails.\n */\nssize_t cg_open(cg_key_t      *key,\n                const uint8_t *cipher,\n                size_t         cipher_len,\n                uint8_t       *out_plain,\n                size_t         out_plain_sz);\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Secure memory hygiene                                                    */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/* Overwrite |len| bytes starting at |ptr| even under aggressive optimisation */\nvoid cg_secure_bzero(void *ptr, size_t len);\n\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*  Key management (teaching mode)                                           */\n/* ------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/**\n * Acquires an AES-256-GCM key.\n *   • Production:  pulled from HSM via PKCS#11.\n *   • Lab mode:    derived from EDUPAY_COMPLIANCE_KEY (64-hex chars) or\n *                  filled with /dev/urandom.\n */\ncg_key_t *cg_key_acquire(void);\n\n/* Zeroise and free key material */\nvoid cg_key_release(cg_key_t *key);\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\n}\n#endif\n#endif /* EDU_COMPLIANCE_GUARD_H */\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2 ╱ 4 Reference implementation (`compliance_guard.c`)\n\n```c\n/*──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  compliance_guard.c  —  Internal library for the Compliance micro-service\n──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────*/\n#include \"compliance_guard.h\"\n\n#include <openssl/evp.h>\n#include <openssl/rand.h>\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n\n#define CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN   12u\n#define CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN  16u\n#define CG_PAN_MAX_LEN      19u     /* ISO/IEC 7812-1 */\n#define CG_TOKEN_LEN        19u\n\nstruct cg_key { uint8_t key[32]; };\n\n/* ───────────────────────────── Internal helpers ─────────────────────────── */\n\nstatic int cg_generate_iv(uint8_t *iv, size_t iv_len)\n{\n    return RAND_bytes(iv, (int)iv_len) == 1 ? 0 : -EIO;\n}\n\nstatic int cg_validate_luhn(const char *digits)\n{\n    size_t len = strlen(digits);\n    int sum = 0, alt = 0;\n\n    for (ssize_t i = (ssize_t)len - 1; i >= 0; --i)\n    {\n        int d = digits[i] - '0';\n        if (d < 0 || d > 9) return -EINVAL;\n        if (alt) { d *= 2; if (d > 9) d -= 9; }\n        sum += d; alt = !alt;\n    }\n    return sum % 10 == 0 ? 0 : -EINVAL;\n}\n\nstatic uint8_t cg_rand_digit(void)\n{\n    uint8_t b; RAND_bytes(&b, 1); return (uint8_t)(b % 10);\n}\n\nstatic int cg_build_token(const char *pan, char *tok)\n{\n    size_t len = strlen(pan);\n    if (len < 8 || len > CG_PAN_MAX_LEN) return -EINVAL;\n\n    memcpy(tok, pan, 6);                 /* Keep BIN for analytics */\n    for (size_t i = 6; i < len - 1; ++i) /* Randomise body         */\n        tok[i] = '0' + cg_rand_digit();\n\n    /* Recalculate Luhn check digit */\n    int sum = 0, alt = 1;\n    for (ssize_t i = (ssize_t)len - 2; i >= 0; --i)\n    {\n        int d = tok[i] - '0';\n        if (alt) { d *= 2; if (d > 9) d -= 9; }\n        sum += d; alt = !alt;\n    }\n    tok[len - 1] = '0' + ((10 - (sum % 10)) % 10);\n    tok[len] = '\\0';\n    return 0;\n}\n\n/* ───────────────────────────── Public API ───────────────────────────────── */\n\nint cg_tokenise_pan(const char *pan, char *out_token, size_t out_len)\n{\n    if (!pan || !out_token)                 return -EINVAL;\n    const size_t n = strlen(pan);\n    if (n == 0 || n > CG_PAN_MAX_LEN)       return -EINVAL;\n    if (out_len < n + 1)                    return -ENOSPC;\n    if (cg_validate_luhn(pan) != 0)         return -EINVAL;\n\n    /* Switchable algorithm for classroom demos */\n    if (getenv(\"EDUPAY_FPE\") && *getenv(\"EDUPAY_FPE\") == '1')\n    {\n        /* Placeholder for format-preserving AES-FF3-1 */\n        return cg_build_token(pan, out_token); /* Fallback path */\n    }\n    return cg_build_token(pan, out_token);\n}\n\nssize_t cg_seal(cg_key_t      *key,\n                const uint8_t *pt,\n                size_t         pt_len,\n                uint8_t       *out,\n                size_t         out_sz)\n{\n    if (!key || !pt || !out || pt_len == 0)  return -EINVAL;\n    const size_t need = CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN + pt_len + CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN;\n    if (out_sz < need)                       return -ENOSPC;\n\n    uint8_t *iv     = out;\n    uint8_t *cipher = out + CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN;\n    uint8_t *tag    = cipher + pt_len;\n\n    if (cg_generate_iv(iv, CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN) != 0) return -EIO;\n\n    ssize_t ret = -EIO;\n    EVP_CIPHER_CTX *ctx = EVP_CIPHER_CTX_new();\n    if (!ctx) return -ENOMEM;\n\n    do {\n        if (EVP_EncryptInit_ex(ctx, EVP_aes_256_gcm(), NULL, NULL, NULL) != 1) break;\n        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_SET_IVLEN,\n                                CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN, NULL) != 1)               break;\n        if (EVP_EncryptInit_ex(ctx, NULL, NULL, key->key, iv) != 1)           break;\n\n        int len = 0, total = 0;\n        if (EVP_EncryptUpdate(ctx, cipher, &len, pt, (int)pt_len) != 1)       break;\n        total += len;\n        if (EVP_EncryptFinal_ex(ctx, cipher + total, &len) != 1)              break;\n        total += len;\n        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_GET_TAG,\n                                CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN, tag) != 1)               break;\n\n        ret = CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN + total + CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN;\n    } while (0);\n\n    EVP_CIPHER_CTX_free(ctx);\n    return ret;\n}\n\nssize_t cg_open(cg_key_t      *key,\n                const uint8_t *in,\n                size_t         in_len,\n                uint8_t       *out,\n                size_t         out_sz)\n{\n    if (!key || !in || !out)                         return -EINVAL;\n    if (in_len < CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN + CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN) return -EINVAL;\n\n    const uint8_t *iv     = in;\n    const uint8_t *cipher = in + CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN;\n    const size_t   ct_len = in_len - CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN - CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN;\n    const uint8_t *tag    = in + in_len - CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN;\n\n    if (out_sz < ct_len)                             return -ENOSPC;\n\n    ssize_t ret = -EIO;\n    EVP_CIPHER_CTX *ctx = EVP_CIPHER_CTX_new();\n    if (!ctx) return -ENOMEM;\n\n    do {\n        if (EVP_DecryptInit_ex(ctx, EVP_aes_256_gcm(), NULL, NULL, NULL) != 1) break;\n        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_SET_IVLEN,\n                                CG_AES_GCM_IV_LEN, NULL) != 1)               break;\n        if (EVP_DecryptInit_ex(ctx, NULL, NULL, key->key, iv) != 1)           break;\n\n        int len = 0, total = 0;\n        if (EVP_DecryptUpdate(ctx, out, &len, cipher, (int)ct_len) != 1)      break;\n        total += len;\n\n        if (EVP_CIPHER_CTX_ctrl(ctx, EVP_CTRL_GCM_SET_TAG,\n                                CG_AES_GCM_TAG_LEN, (void *)tag) != 1)       break;\n        if (EVP_DecryptFinal_ex(ctx, out + total, &len) != 1) {\n            ret = -EBADMSG;                                                  break;\n        }\n        total += len;\n        ret = total;\n    } while (0);\n\n    EVP_CIPHER_CTX_free(ctx);\n    return ret;\n}\n\nvoid cg_secure_bzero(void *ptr, size_t len)\n{\n    if (!ptr || !len) return;\n#if defined(__STDC_LIB_EXT1__)\n    memset_s(ptr, len, 0, len);\n#else\n    volatile uint8_t *p = (volatile uint8_t *)ptr;\n    while (len--) *p++ = 0;\n#endif\n}\n\ncg_key_t *cg_key_acquire(void)\n{\n    cg_key_t *k = calloc(1, sizeof *k);\n    if (!k) return NULL;\n\n    const char *env = getenv(\"EDUPAY_COMPLIANCE_KEY\");\n    if (env && strlen(env) == 64)\n    {   /* Hex-decode deterministic, instructor-supplied key */\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < 32; ++i)\n            sscanf(&env[i * 2], \"%2hhx\", &k->key[i]);\n    }\n    else if (RAND_bytes(k->key, sizeof k->key) != 1)\n    {\n        free(k); k = NULL;\n    }\n    return k;\n}\n\nvoid cg_key_release(cg_key_t *key)\n{\n    if (!key) return;\n    cg_secure_bzero(key->key, sizeof key->key);\n    free(key);\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3 ╱ 4 End-to-end console demo (`example_usage.c`)\n\n```c\n/* Compile with\n *   cc -std=c11 -Wall -Wextra example_usage.c compliance_guard.c -lcrypto -o demo\n * Run with\n *   EDUPAY_COMPLIANCE_KEY=$(openssl rand -hex 32) ./demo\n */\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <string.h>\n\n#include \"compliance_guard.h\"\n\nint main(void)\n{\n    const char *pan = \"4485275742308327\"; /* Visa test card */\n\n    char token[CG_TOKEN_LEN + 1];\n    if (cg_tokenise_pan(pan, token, sizeof token) != 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Tokenisation failed\\n\");\n        return 1;\n    }\n    printf(\"Tokenised PAN  : %s\\n\", token);\n\n    cg_key_t *key = cg_key_acquire();\n    if (!key) { perror(\"key_acquire\"); return 1; }\n\n    uint8_t cipher[256];\n    ssize_t n_cipher = cg_seal(key,\n                               (const uint8_t *)token, strlen(token),\n                               cipher, sizeof cipher);\n    cg_secure_bzero(token, sizeof token); /* Immediately destroy clear-text */\n    if (n_cipher < 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Encryption failed: %zd\\n\", n_cipher);\n        cg_key_release(key);\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    uint8_t clear[64];\n    ssize_t n_plain = cg_open(key, cipher, (size_t)n_cipher,\n                              clear, sizeof clear);\n    cg_key_release(key);\n\n    if (n_plain < 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"Decryption failed: %zd\\n\", n_plain);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    printf(\"Decrypted token: %.*s\\n\", (int)n_plain, clear);\n    cg_secure_bzero(clear, (size_t)n_plain);\n\n    return 0;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4 ╱ 4 Lab exercises\n\n1. Extend `cg_tokenise_pan()` to call an FF3-1 library when `EDUPAY_FPE=1`.  \n   Verify that the output still passes Luhn and preserves BIN/IIN.\n\n2. Attach `cg_seal()` to the CQRS → Audit-Trail write model.  \n   Use Wireshark to confirm that events on the message bus no longer contain\n   clear-text PAN or student identifiers (FERPA §99.31).\n\n3. Fire up `valgrind --tool=memcheck ./demo` and inspect the heap after\n   `cg_secure_bzero()` executes. No clear-text secrets should appear.\n\n---\n\n© 2024 EduPay Ledger Academy — MIT License","numLines":408,"startLine":1,"totalLines":408}}}

In production you would normally split\n *          declarations (.h) from definitions (.c).\n */\n\n#ifndef EDUPAY_LEDGER_ACADEMY_DASHBOARD_PROJECTOR_H\n#define EDUPAY_LEDGER_ACADEMY_DASHBOARD_PROJECTOR_H\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*  Standard Library                                                         */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n#include <assert.h>\n#include <errno.h>\n#include <inttypes.h>\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <stdbool.h>\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <stdlib.h>\n#include <string.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*  Third-Party Dependencies                                                 */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*  We depend only on SQLite, an ubiquitous, embeddable RDBMS.               */\n#include <sqlite3.h>\n\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n/*  Public API                                                               */\n/* ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\n#ifdef __cplusplus\nextern \"C\" {\n#endif\n\n/* Ledger event types that the projector knows how to fold.                  */\ntypedef enum\n{\n    EVT_UNDEFINED = 0,\n    EVT_PAYMENT_PROCESSED,\n    EVT_TRANSACTION_SETTLED,\n    EVT_FRAUD_FLAGGED,\n    EVT_CURRENCY_CONVERTED,\n    EVT_SCHOLARSHIP_DISBURSED\n} ledger_event_type_t;\n\n/* Immutable envelope received from the event-store / broker.                */\ntypedef struct\n{\n    ledger_event_type_t type;      /* Event discriminator.                  */\n    const char         *payload;   /* UTF-8 JSON blob (null-terminated).    */\n    uint64_t            sequence;  /* Global ordering, monotonic.           */\n    uint64_t            ts_epoch_ms; /* Milliseconds since Unix epoch.      */\n} ledger_event_t;\n\n/* Opaque handle exposed to application code.                                */\ntypedef struct\n{\n    sqlite3        *db;            /* Connection to read-model store.       */\n    pthread_mutex_t mtx;           /* Serializes concurrent folds.          */\n} dashboard_projector_t;\n\n/* Construction / teardown. already defined elsewhere\"\n#endif\n#define DASHBOARD_PROJECTOR_IMPLEMENTATION 1\n\n/* SQLite helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\nstatic inline void _log_sqlite_error(const char *ctx, sqlite3 *db)\n{\n    fprintf(stderr, \"[dashboard_projector] %s: %s (code=%d)\\n\",\n            ctx, sqlite3_errmsg(db), sqlite3_errcode(db));\n}\n\nstatic inline bool _exec(sqlite3 *db, const char *sql)\n{\n    char *errmsg = NULL;\n    if (sqlite3_exec(db, sql, NULL, NULL, &errmsg) != SQLITE_OK)\n    {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"[dashboard_projector] SQL error: %s\\n\", errmsg);\n        sqlite3_free(errmsg);\n        return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\n/* Schema bootstrap ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\nstatic const char *_DDL[] = {\n    /* Metadata used for idempotent re-playing (at-least-once deliveries).   */\n    \"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS projection_metadata (\"\n    \"  id                INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),\"\n    \"  last_sequence     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0\"\n    \");\"\n    \"INSERT OR IGNORE INTO projection_metadata (id, last_sequence) VALUES (1, 0);\",\n\n    /* Highly-denormalized table powering the dashboard widgets.             */\n    \"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS dashboard_snapshot (\"\n    \"  id                  INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK (id = 1),\"\n    \"  total_payments_usd  REAL    NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0,\"\n    \"  total_settlements   REAL    NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0,\"\n    \"  fraud_incidents     INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\"\n    \"  scholarship_outflow REAL    NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0\"\n    \");\"\n    \"INSERT OR IGNORE INTO dashboard_snapshot (id) VALUES (1);\",\n\n    NULL /* sentinel */\n};\n\nstatic inline bool _ensure_schema(sqlite3 *db)\n{\n    for (const char **ddl = _DDL; *ddl; ++ddl)\n        if (!_exec(db, *ddl))\n            return false;\n    return true;\n}\n\n/* Primitive JSON parsing utilities (no external dependency).                */\n/* Security: Not a full JSON parser — good enough for our limited shapes.    */\n\nstatic inline bool _json_extract_double(const char *json,\n                                        const char *key,\n                                        double     *out_val)\n{\n    if (!json || !key || !out_val) return false;\n\n    const size_t key_len = strlen(key);\n    const char  *pos     = json;\n    while ((pos = strstr(pos, key)))\n    {\n        pos += key_len;\n        /* Skip whitespace, colon, whitespace */\n        while (*pos && (*pos == ' ' || *pos == '\\t' || *pos == '\\n' ||\n                        *pos == '\\r' || *pos == ':'))\n            ++pos;\n        if (!*pos) break;\n\n        /* +/-? digits?. */\n        char *endptr = NULL;\n        errno = 0;\n        double val  = strtod(pos, &endptr);\n        if (endptr != pos && errno == 0)\n        {\n            *out_val = val;\n            return true;\n        }\n        /* Else: continue searching (false hit). */\n    }\n    return false;\n}\n\n/* Fetch last processed sequence to achieve idempotency.                     */\nstatic inline uint64_t _get_last_sequence(sqlite3 *db)\n{\n    sqlite3_stmt *stmt = NULL;\n    uint64_t      seq  = 0;\n\n    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(db,\n                           \"SELECT last_sequence FROM projection_metadata \"\n                           \"WHERE id = 1;\",\n                           -1, &stmt, NULL) == SQLITE_OK)\n    {\n        if (sqlite3_step(stmt) == SQLITE_ROW)\n            seq = (uint64_t)sqlite3_column_int64(stmt, 0);\n    }\n    else\n        _log_sqlite_error(\"_get_last_sequence/prepare\", db);\n\n    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n    return seq;\n}\n\nstatic inline bool _update_last_sequence(sqlite3 *db, uint64_t seq)\n{\n    sqlite3_stmt *stmt = NULL;\n    bool          ok   = false;\n\n    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(db,\n                           \"UPDATE projection_metadata SET last_sequence = ?\"\n                           \" WHERE id = 1;\",\n                           -1, &stmt, NULL) == SQLITE_OK)\n    {\n        sqlite3_bind_int64(stmt, 1, (sqlite3_int64)seq);\n        ok = (sqlite3_step(stmt) == SQLITE_DONE);\n    }\n    else\n        _log_sqlite_error(\"_update_last_sequence/prepare\", db);\n\n    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n    return ok;\n}\n\n/* Public functions ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */\n\nbool dashboard_projector_init(dashboard_projector_t *prj,\n                              const char            *sqlite_path)\n{\n    if (!prj || !sqlite_path)\n    {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"[dashboard_projector] init: invalid arguments\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    memset(prj, 0, sizeof(*prj));\n\n    /* Open SQLite in WAL mode for concurrency friendliness.                 */\n    int rc = sqlite3_open_v2(sqlite_path,\n                             &prj->db,\n                             SQLITE_OPEN_READWRITE | SQLITE_OPEN_CREATE |\n                                 SQLITE_OPEN_FULLMUTEX,\n                             NULL);\n    if (rc != SQLITE_OK)\n    {\n        _log_sqlite_error(\"sqlite3_open_v2\", prj->db);\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    if (!_ensure_schema(prj->db))\n        goto fail;\n\n    if (pthread_mutex_init(&prj->mtx, NULL) != 0)\n    {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"[dashboard_projector] pthread_mutex_init failed\\n\");\n        goto fail;\n    }\n\n    /* Switch to WAL for perf. */\n    if (!_exec(prj->db, \"PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;\"))\n        goto fail_mutex;\n\n    return true;\n\n/* Error unwind helpers */\nfail_mutex:\n    pthread_mutex_destroy(&prj->mtx);\nfail:\n    sqlite3_close(prj->db);\n    prj->db = NULL;\n    return false;\n}\n\nvoid dashboard_projector_shutdown(dashboard_projector_t *prj)\n{\n    if (!prj || !prj->db) return;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&prj->mtx);\n    sqlite3_close(prj->db);\n    prj->db = NULL;\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&prj->mtx);\n\n    pthread_mutex_destroy(&prj->mtx);\n}\n\n/* Actual fold implementation per event type.                                */\n\nstatic inline bool _fold_payment_processed(sqlite3 *db, double amount_usd)\n{\n    sqlite3_stmt *stmt = NULL;\n    bool          ok   = false;\n\n    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(\n            db,\n            \"UPDATE dashboard_snapshot \"\n            \"SET total_payments_usd = total_payments_usd + ? \"\n            \"WHERE id = 1;\",\n            -1, &stmt, NULL) == SQLITE_OK)\n    {\n        sqlite3_bind_double(stmt, 1, amount_usd);\n        ok = (sqlite3_step(stmt) == SQLITE_DONE);\n    }\n    else\n        _log_sqlite_error(\"_fold_payment_processed/prepare\", db);\n\n    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n    return ok;\n}\n\nstatic inline bool _fold_transaction_settled(sqlite3 *db, double amount)\n{\n    sqlite3_stmt *stmt = NULL;\n    bool          ok   = false;\n\n    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(\n            db,\n            \"UPDATE dashboard_snapshot \"\n            \"SET total_settlements = total_settlements + ? \"\n            \"WHERE id = 1;\",\n            -1, &stmt, NULL) == SQLITE_OK)\n    {\n        sqlite3_bind_double(stmt, 1, amount);\n        ok = (sqlite3_step(stmt) == SQLITE_DONE);\n    }\n    else\n        _log_sqlite_error(\"_fold_transaction_settled/prepare\", db);\n\n    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n    return ok;\n}\n\nstatic inline bool _fold_fraud_flagged(sqlite3 *db)\n{\n    return _exec(db,\n                 \"UPDATE dashboard_snapshot \"\n                 \"SET fraud_incidents = fraud_incidents + 1 \"\n                 \"WHERE id = 1;\");\n}\n\nstatic inline bool _fold_scholarship_disbursed(sqlite3 *db, double amount)\n{\n    sqlite3_stmt *stmt = NULL;\n    bool          ok   = false;\n\n    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(\n            db,\n            \"UPDATE dashboard_snapshot \"\n            \"SET scholarship_outflow = scholarship_outflow + ? \"\n            \"WHERE id = 1;\",\n            -1, &stmt, NULL) == SQLITE_OK)\n    {\n        sqlite3_bind_double(stmt, 1, amount);\n        ok = (sqlite3_step(stmt) == SQLITE_DONE);\n    }\n    else\n        _log_sqlite_error(\"_fold_scholarship_disbursed/prepare\", db);\n\n    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n    return ok;\n}\n\n/* Main folding orchestrator                                                 */\n\nvoid dashboard_projector_handle_event(dashboard_projector_t *prj,\n                                      const ledger_event_t  *evt)\n{\n    if (!prj || !evt || !prj->db) return;\n\n    pthread_mutex_lock(&prj->mtx);\n\n    /* Idempotent replay guard.                                              */\n    uint64_t last_seq = _get_last_sequence(prj->db);\n    if (evt->sequence <= last_seq)\n    {\n        pthread_mutex_unlock(&prj->mtx);\n        return; /* Already processed. */\n    }\n\n    /* Begin transaction.                                                    */\n    if (!_exec(prj->db, \"BEGIN;\"))\n        goto unlock;\n\n    bool fold_ok  = false;\n    double amount = 0.0;\n\n    switch (evt->type)\n    {\n        case EVT_PAYMENT_PROCESSED:\n            if (_json_extract_double(evt->payload, \"\\\"amount\\\"\", &amount))\n                fold_ok = _fold_payment_processed(prj->db, amount);\n            break;\n\n        case EVT_TRANSACTION_SETTLED:\n            if (_json_extract_double(evt->payload, \"\\\"amount\\\"\", &amount))\n                fold_ok = _fold_transaction_settled(prj->db, amount);\n            break;\n\n        case EVT_FRAUD_FLAGGED:\n            fold_ok = _fold_fraud_flagged(prj->db);\n            break;\n\n        case EVT_SCHOLARSHIP_DISBURSED:\n            if (_json_extract_double(evt->payload, \"\\\"amount\\\"\", &amount))\n                fold_ok = _fold_scholarship_disbursed(prj->db, amount);\n            break;\n\n        /* Events not relevant for the dashboard are safely ignored.         */\n        default:\n            fold_ok = true;\n            break;\n    }\n\n    /* Update checkpoint if the fold succeeded.                              */\n    if (fold_ok)\n        fold_ok = _update_last_sequence(prj->db, evt->sequence);\n\n    /* Commit or rollback.                                                   */\n    if (fold_ok)\n    {\n        if (!_exec(prj->db, \"COMMIT;\"))\n            _exec(prj->db, \"ROLLBACK;\");\n    }\n    else\n    {\n        _exec(prj->db, \"ROLLBACK;\");\n    }\n\nunlock:\n    pthread_mutex_unlock(&prj->mtx);\n}\n\n#endif /* EDUPAY_LEDGER_ACADEMY_DASHBOARD_PROJECTOR_H */\n","numLines":430,"startLine":1,"totalLines":430}}}

Each BC is\n    16→implemented as one or more micro-services—small autonomous executables\n    17→written in C and packaged as OCI containers.\n    18→\n    19→| Bounded Context         | Canonical Service | Purpose                                                                    |\n    20→|-------------------------|-------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------------------|\n    21→| Admissions              | `admissions-api`  | On-boards students, allocates student-ids, emits *StudentRegistered*       |\n    22→| Bursar (A/R)            | `ledger-core`     | Double-entry ledger, tuition invoicing, GL postings, payment gateway       |\n    23→| Financial-Aid           | `aid-engine`      | Award rules, stipend disbursement, compliance (FERPA / PSD2 / PCI-DSS)     |\n    24→| Continuing-Education    | `micro-cred`      | Per-course micro-credential tracking, pay-per-module split-payments         |\n    25→\n    26→A **Shared-Kernel** provides value-objects (`Money`, `Currency`,\n    27→`StudentId`) and the **AuditTrail** event store.\n    28→\n    29→```\n    30→                        +-----------+\n    31→                        |  API-GW   |\n    32→                        +-----------+\n    33→                              |\n    34→            +-----------------+------------------+\n    35→            |                 |                  |\n    36→    +--------------+  +---------------+    50→## Event Catalog (Ubiquitous Language)\n    51→\n    52→* `StudentRegistered`\n    53→* `InvoiceRaised`\n    54→* `PaymentAuthorized`\n    55→* `AidDisbursed`\n    56→* `LedgerEntryPosted`\n    57→* `SagaCompensationRequired`\n    58→\n    59→### Example Domain Event (C, Protobuf-ish Header)\n    60→\n    61→```c\n    62→/* events/payment_authorized.h */\n    63→#ifndef EP_EVENTS_PAYMENT_AUTH_H\n    64→#define EP_EVENTS_PAYMENT_AUTH_H\n    65→/*\n    66→  Domain Event: PaymentAuthorized\n    67→  This event is emitted by ledger-core after the acquiring bank\n    68→  responds with an authorization code.  Other contexts (Financial-Aid,\n    69→  Continuing-Education) subscribe to project read models.\n    70→*/\n    71→#include <stdint.h>\n    72→#include <time.h>\n    73→\n    74→#define PAYMENT_AUTH_VERSION 1\n    75→\n    76→typedef struct ep_payment_auth_t {\n    77→    uint8_t  version;          /* schema version                     */\n    78→    char     id[37];           /* UUID v4 string                     */\n    79→    char     student_id[16];   /* canonical SID                      */\n    80→    char     invoice_id[37];   /* payment belongs to which invoice   */\n    81→    char     currency[4];      /* ISO-4217                           */\n    82→    int64_t  amount_minor;     /* 1099 = $10.99 ( if cents )         */\n    83→    char     auth_code[12];    /* provided by payment processor      */\n    84→    time_t   occurred_at_utc;  /* RFC 3339 ts                        */\n    85→} ep_payment_auth_t;\n    86→\n    87→#endif /* EP_EVENTS_PAYMENT_AUTH_H */\n    88→```\n    89→\n    90→## Service Skeleton: `ledger-core`\n    91→\n    92→The canonical micro-service exposes gRPC, publishes events on NATS,\n    93→and persists to PostgreSQL.  Internally, Clean Architecture layers are\n    94→segregated into `domain`, `usecase`, and `infra` directories.\n    95→\n    96→### `domain/money.h`\n    97→\n    98→```c\n    99→/* domain/money.h */\n   100→#ifndef EP_DOMAIN_MONEY_H\n   101→#define EP_DOMAIN_MONEY_H\n   102→\n   103→#include <stdint.h>\n   104→\n   105→typedef struct ep_money_t {\n   106→    int64_t amount_minor;  /* -922337203685477 */\n   107→    char    currency[4];   /* \"USD\"            */\n   108→} ep_money_t;\n   109→\n   110→/* Equality check without floating-point errors */\n   111→static inline int ep_money_eq(const ep_money_t *a, const ep_money_t *b)\n   112→{\n   113→    return a && b &&\n   114→           a->amount_minor == b->amount_minor &&\n   115→           a->currency[0] == b->currency[0] &&\n   116→           a->currency[1] == b->currency[1] &&\n   117→           a->currency[2] == b->currency[2] &&\n   118→           a->currency[3] == b->currency[3];\n   119→}\n Idempotent command handlers.\n   279→2. At-least-once event delivery (NATS JetStream).\n   280→3. Circuit-breaker around external PSP, open after 3 failures / 30 s.\n   281→4. Saga compensation for inter-context failure.\n   282→\n   283→```c\n   284→/* infra/circuit_breaker.h */\n   285→#ifndef EP_INFRA_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_H\n   286→#define EP_INFRA_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_H\n   287→#include <time.h>\n   288→typedef enum { CB_CLOSED, CB_OPEN, CB_HALF_OPEN } cb_state_t;\n   289→\n   290→typedef struct circuit_breaker {\n   291→    cb_state_t state;\n   292→    unsigned   failure_count;\n   293→    time_t     last_failure;\n   294→    unsigned   open_timeout_sec;\n   295→    unsigned   threshold;\n   296→} circuit_breaker_t;\n   297→\n   298→void cb_init(circuit_breaker_t *cb,\n   299→             unsigned threshold,\n   300→             unsigned open_timeout_sec);\n   301→\n   302→int  cb_before_request(circuit_breaker_t *cb);\n   303→void cb_after_success(circuit_breaker_t *cb);\n   304→void cb_after_failure(circuit_breaker_t *cb);\n   305→\n   306→#endif /* EP_INFRA_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_H */\n   307→```\n   308→\n   309→## Build & Deployment Standards\n   310→\n   311→* `Dockerfile` uses scratch + `musl-clang` static linking ⇒ 6 MB images.\n   312→* Each service exports `/livez` and `/readyz` over HTTP on port 8080.\n   313→* Kubernetes Helm charts pin CPU/memory, configure HPA on NATS lag.\n   314→\n   315→---\n   316→\n   317→> “Architecture is about the important stuff—whatever that is.”  \n   318→> — Ralph Johnson\n   319→\n   320→Learners should fork this repository, enable **Saga Pattern\n   321→Demonstration Mode** (`make chaos-on`), and trigger the `tuition\n   322→checkout` flow. (clang ‑std=c17 ‑Wall ‑Wextra\n-pedantic ‑O2 listing.c ‑o listing && ./listing)\n-->\n\n# Micro-services & Bounded Contexts\n\nEduPay Ledger Academy follows Domain-Driven Design and splits the\nbusiness domain into **four bounded contexts** (BCs).  Each BC is\nimplemented as one or more micro-services—small autonomous executables\nwritten in C and packaged as OCI containers.\n\n| Bounded Context         | Canonical Service | Purpose                                                                    |\n|-------------------------|-------------------|----------------------------------------------------------------------------|\n| Admissions              | `admissions-api`  | On-boards students, allocates student-ids, emits *StudentRegistered*       |\n| Bursar (A/R)            | `ledger-core`     | Double-entry ledger, tuition invoicing, GL postings, payment gateway       |\n| Financial-Aid           | `aid-engine`      | Award rules, stipend disbursement, compliance (FERPA / PSD2 / PCI-DSS)     |\n| Continuing-Education    | `micro-cred`      | Per-course micro-credential tracking, pay-per-module split-payments         |\n\nA **Shared-Kernel** provides value-objects (`Money`, `Currency`,\n`StudentId`) and the **AuditTrail** event store.\n\n```\n                        +-----------+\n                        |  API-GW   |\n                        +-----------+\n                              |\n            +-----------------+------------------+\n            |                 |                  |\n    +--------------+  +---------------+  +---------------+\n    | admissions   |  | ledger-core   |  | aid-engine    |\n    +--------------+  +---------------+  +---------------+\n            \\           /       \\              /\n             \\         /         \\            /\n              \\       /           \\          /\n              +----- Event Bus (NATS / Kafka) -+\n                        ^\n                        |\n                 +---------------+\n                 |  AuditTrail   |\n                 +---------------+\n```\n\n## Event Catalog (Ubiquitous Language)\n\n* `StudentRegistered`\n* `InvoiceRaised`\n* `PaymentAuthorized`\n* `AidDisbursed`\n* `LedgerEntryPosted`\n* `SagaCompensationRequired`\n\n### Example Domain Event (C, Protobuf-ish Header)\n\n```c\n/* events/payment_authorized.h */\n#ifndef EP_EVENTS_PAYMENT_AUTH_H\n#define EP_EVENTS_PAYMENT_AUTH_H\n/*\n  Domain Event: PaymentAuthorized\n  This event is emitted by ledger-core after the acquiring bank\n  responds with an authorization code.  Other contexts (Financial-Aid,\n  Continuing-Education) subscribe to project read models.\n*/\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\n#define PAYMENT_AUTH_VERSION 1\n\ntypedef struct ep_payment_auth_t {\n    uint8_t  version;          /* schema version                     */\n    char     id[37];           /* UUID v4 string                     */\n    char     student_id[16];   /* canonical SID                      */\n    char     invoice_id[37];   /* payment belongs to which invoice   */\n    char     currency[4];      /* ISO-4217                           */\n    int64_t  amount_minor;     /* 1099 = $10.99 ( if cents )         */\n    char     auth_code[12];    /* provided by payment processor      */\n    time_t   occurred_at_utc;  /* RFC 3339 ts                        */\n} ep_payment_auth_t;\n\n#endif /* EP_EVENTS_PAYMENT_AUTH_H */\n```\n\n## Service Skeleton: `ledger-core`\n\nThe canonical micro-service exposes gRPC, publishes events on NATS,\nand persists to PostgreSQL.  Internally, Clean Architecture layers are\nsegregated into `domain`, `usecase`, and `infra` directories.\n\n### `domain/money.h`\n\n```c\n/* domain/money.h */\n#ifndef EP_DOMAIN_MONEY_H\n#define EP_DOMAIN_MONEY_H\n\n#include <stdint.h>\n\ntypedef struct ep_money_t {\n    int64_t amount_minor;  /* -922337203685477 */\n    char    currency[4];   /* \"USD\"            */\n} ep_money_t;\n\n/* Equality check without floating-point errors */\nstatic inline int ep_money_eq(const ep_money_t *a, const ep_money_t *b)\n{\n    return a && b &&\n           a->amount_minor == b->amount_minor &&\n           a->currency[0] == b->currency[0] &&\n           a->currency[1] == b->currency[1] &&\n           a->currency[2] == b->currency[2] &&\n           a->currency[3] == b->currency[3];\n}\n\n#endif /* EP_DOMAIN_MONEY_H */\n```\n\n### `usecase/post_payment.c`\n\n```c\n/* usecase/post_payment.c */\n#include \"domain/money.h\"\n#include \"events/payment_authorized.h\"\n#include <stdio.h>\n#include <string.h>\n\ntypedef struct ep_payment_repo ep_payment_repo_t;\ntypedef struct ep_event_bus   ep_event_bus_t;\n\n/* Dependency inversion via pure C interfaces */\nstruct ep_payment_repo {\n    int (*begin_tx)(ep_payment_repo_t *);\n    int (*commit_tx)(ep_payment_repo_t *);\n    int (*rollback_tx)(ep_payment_repo_t *);\n    int (*insert_ledger_entry)(ep_payment_repo_t *,\n                               const char  *invoice_id,\n                               const ep_money_t *amount,\n                               const char  *auth_code);\n};\n\nstruct ep_event_bus {\n    int (*publish)(ep_event_bus_t *, const void *evt, size_t sz);\n};\n\n/*\n  Post a payment to the double-entry ledger.\n\n  Returns 0 on success, < 0 on domain error, > 0 on infra error.\n*/\nint ep_uc_post_payment(ep_payment_repo_t *repo,\n                       ep_event_bus_t    *bus,\n                       const char        *invoice_id,\n                       const ep_money_t  *amount,\n                       const char        *auth_code)\n{\n    if (!repo || !bus || !invoice_id || !amount || !auth_code)\n        return -1; /* pre-condition failed */\n\n    if (repo->begin_tx(repo) != 0)\n        return  1; /* infra error */\n\n    if (repo->insert_ledger_entry(repo, invoice_id, amount, auth_code) != 0) {\n        repo->rollback_tx(repo);\n        return  2;\n    }\n\n    ep_payment_auth_t evt = {\n        .version        = PAYMENT_AUTH_VERSION,\n        .occurred_at_utc= time(NULL),\n    };\n    /* (Safe) string copy utilities omitted for brevity */\n    strncpy(evt.id,        \"UUID-GEN-STUB\", sizeof evt.id);\n    strncpy(evt.student_id,\"SID-LOOKUP-X\", sizeof evt.student_id);\n    strncpy(evt.invoice_id,invoice_id,     sizeof evt.invoice_id);\n    strncpy(evt.currency,  amount->currency, sizeof evt.currency);\n    evt.amount_minor = amount->amount_minor;\n    strncpy(evt.auth_code, auth_code, sizeof evt.auth_code);\n\n    if (bus->publish(bus, &evt, sizeof evt) != 0) {\n        repo->rollback_tx(repo);\n        return  3;\n    }\n\n    return repo->commit_tx(repo);\n}\n```\n\n### Saga Orchestrator (Distributed Tuition Payment)\n\n```c\n/* saga/tuition_payment_saga.h */\n#ifndef EP_SAGA_TUITION_PAYMENT_H\n#define EP_SAGA_TUITION_PAYMENT_H\n\n/*\n  TuitionPaymentSaga orchestrates:\n\n  1. Reserve Financial-Aid budget\n  2. Authorize card payment (Bursar)\n  3. Disburse stipends\n  4. Issue micro-credential revenue split\n\n  Compensation order is reverse.\n\n  State machine is stored in the EventStore; students step through\n  failures during outage simulations.\n*/\n\ntypedef enum {\n    SAGA_STEP_NONE = 0,\n    SAGA_STEP_AID_RESERVED,\n    SAGA_STEP_PAYMENT_AUTHORIZED,\n    SAGA_STEP_STIPEND_PAID,\n    SAGA_STEP_MICROCRED_SPLIT,\n    SAGA_STEP_DONE,\n    SAGA_STEP_FAILED\n} ep_saga_state_t;\n\n#endif /* EP_SAGA_TUITION_PAYMENT_H */\n```\n\n## Anti-corruption Layer (ACL) Sample\n\nAdmissions uses an external vendor for SIS and must translate its JSON\nschema into *StudentRegistered* events.\n\n```c\n/* acl/sis_adapter.c */\n#include <jansson.h>\n#include \"events/student_registered.h\"\n\n/* Returns 0 on success */\nint ep_acl_translate_sis_event(const char *json,\n                               ep_student_reg_t *out_evt)\n{\n    json_error_t err;\n    json_t *root = json_loads(json, 0, &err);\n    if (!root) return -1;\n\n    const char *sid   = json_string_value(json_object_get(root, \"sid\"));\n    const char *email = json_string_value(json_object_get(root, \"email\"));\n    if (!sid || !email) {\n        json_decref(root);\n        return -2;\n    }\n\n    strncpy(out_evt->student_id, sid, sizeof out_evt->student_id);\n    strncpy(out_evt->email,      email, sizeof out_evt->email);\n    out_evt->occurred_at_utc = time(NULL);\n\n    json_decref(root);\n    return 0;\n}\n```\n\n## Data Ownership & ACID Boundaries\n\n* **Admissions** owns `Student` aggregate; others reference via SID.\n* **Ledger-core** owns `Invoice`, `LedgerEntry`.\n* **Financial-Aid** owns `Award`, `AidDisbursement`.\n* Shared Kernel’s `AuditTrail` is append-only; no service can mutate.\n\n| Service        | DB Schema          | Tx Boundary (Local ACID)    |\n|----------------|--------------------|-----------------------------|\n| admissions     | `admissions.*`     | `Student`, `Applicant`      |\n| ledger-core    | `ledger.*`         | `Invoice`, `LedgerEntry`    |\n| aid-engine     | `aid.*`            | `Award`, `Disbursement`     |\n| micro-cred     | `microcred.*`      | `CourseRun`, `SplitPayout`  |\n\n## Resilience Policy\n\n1. Idempotent command handlers.\n2. At-least-once event delivery (NATS JetStream).\n3. Circuit-breaker around external PSP, open after 3 failures / 30 s.\n4. Saga compensation for inter-context failure.\n\n```c\n/* infra/circuit_breaker.h */\n#ifndef EP_INFRA_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_H\n#define EP_INFRA_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_H\n#include <time.h>\ntypedef enum { CB_CLOSED, CB_OPEN, CB_HALF_OPEN } cb_state_t;\n\ntypedef struct circuit_breaker {\n    cb_state_t state;\n    unsigned   failure_count;\n    time_t     last_failure;\n    unsigned   open_timeout_sec;\n    unsigned   threshold;\n} circuit_breaker_t;\n\nvoid cb_init(circuit_breaker_t *cb,\n             unsigned threshold,\n             unsigned open_timeout_sec);\n\nint  cb_before_request(circuit_breaker_t *cb);\nvoid cb_after_success(circuit_breaker_t *cb);\nvoid cb_after_failure(circuit_breaker_t *cb);\n\n#endif /* EP_INFRA_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_H */\n```\n\n## Build & Deployment Standards\n\n* `Dockerfile` uses scratch + `musl-clang` static linking ⇒ 6 MB images.\n* Each service exports `/livez` and `/readyz` over HTTP on port 8080.\n* Kubernetes Helm charts pin CPU/memory, configure HPA on NATS lag.\n\n---\n\n> “Architecture is about the important stuff—whatever that is.”  \n> — Ralph Johnson\n\nLearners should fork this repository, enable **Saga Pattern\nDemonstration Mode** (`make chaos-on`), and trigger the `tuition\ncheckout` flow.
