The feature automatically applies a student's newly disbursed financial aid stipend towards their outstanding tuition balance, contingent upon explicit opt-in consent from the student.\n\nThe proposed design:\n- **Extends the existing `DisburseStipendSaga`** rather than creating a new saga\n- Maintains strict adherence to **Clean Architecture**, **CQRS/Event Sourcing**, and the **Saga Pattern**\n- Ensures **regulatory compliance** (FERPA, PCI-DSS) through consent-driven workflows\n- Preserves **eventual consistency** across bounded contexts\n\n---\n\n## Table of Contents\n\n1. [Interaction Diagram/Sequence](#1-interaction-diagramsequence)\n2. [Saga Pattern Integration](#2-saga-pattern-integration)\n3. [New Domain Events](#3-new-domain-events)\n4. [CQRS/Event Sourcing Impact](#4-cqrsevent-sourcing-impact)\n5. [Compliance and Consent](#5-compliance-and-consent)\n6. [Affected Components](#6-affected-components)\n7. [Implementation Recommendations](#7-implementation-recommendations)\n\n---\n\n## 1. Interaction Diagram/Sequence\n\n### 1.1 High-Level Sequence Flow\n\nThe Tuition Auto-Payment feature introduces a new step in the stipend disbursement workflow that occurs **after** the stipend is successfully disbursed but **before** the saga completes. Below is the complete sequence:\n\n```\n┌─────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────────────┐    ┌─────────────────────────┐    ┌───────────────────┐    ┌───────────────────┐\n│  Financial Aid  │    │ DisburseStipendSaga     │    │   Projections Service   │    │  Bursar Service   │    │   Event Store     │\n│    Service      │    │   (Orchestrator)        │    │   (Read Model)          │    │  (ledger-core)    │    │  (Postgres/ES)    │\n└────────┬────────┘    └────────────┬────────────┘    └───────────┬─────────────┘    └─────────┬─────────┘    └─────────┬─────────┘\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │ 1. Initiate Saga         │                             │                           │                         │\n         │─────────────────────────>│                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │ 2. validate_eligibility()   │                           │                         │\n         │                          │ (existing step)             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │ 3. reserve_budget()         │                           │                         │\n         │                          │ (existing step)             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │ 4. initiate_transfer()      │                           │                         │\n         │                          │ (existing step)             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │ 5. record_ledger_entry()    │                           │                         │\n         │                          │ (existing step)             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │ 6. publish StipendDisbursed │                           │                         │\n         │                          │ (existing step)             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────>│\n         │                          │                             │                           │       Append Event      │\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │ ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ │\n         │                          │ ║           NEW STEP 7: Apply Stipend to Tuition (Conditional)                  ║ │\n         │                          │ ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ │\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │ 7a. Query: GetAutoPayStatus │                           │                         │\n         │                          │─────────────────────────────>│                           │                         │\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │    {opt_in: true/false,     │                           │                         │\n         │                          │     balance_due: $X}        │                           │                         │\n         │                          │<────────────────────────────│                           │                         │\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │ [IF opt_in=true && balance>0]                           │                         │\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │ 7b. Command: ApplyStipendToTuition                      │                         │\n         │                          │──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────>│                         │\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │                             │                           │ apply_amount =          │\n         │                          │                             │                           │ min(stipend, balance)   │\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │                             │                           │ Debit Student Account   │\n         │                          │                             │                           │ Credit Tuition Account  │\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │                             │                           │ Publish Event           │\n         │                          │                             │                           │────────────────────────>│\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │    {success: true,          │                           │                         │\n         │                          │     applied_amount: $Y}     │                           │                         │\n         │                          │<─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────│                         │\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │ 8. Saga Complete            │                           │                         │\n         │<─────────────────────────│                             │                           │                         │\n         │                          │                             │                           │                         │\n```\n\n### 1.2 Detailed Step Breakdown\n\n| Step | Actor | Action | Description |\n|------|-------|--------|-------------|\n| 1-6 | DisburseStipendSaga | Existing Steps | Validate eligibility, reserve budget, initiate transfer, record ledger, publish `StipendDisbursed` |\n| 7a | Saga Coordinator | Query `GetAutoPaymentStatus` | Queries the projections_service read model for: (1) student's opt-in consent flag, (2) outstanding tuition balance |\n| 7b | Saga Coordinator | Command `ApplyStipendToTuition` | If opt-in=true AND balance>0, sends command to bursar_service |\n| 7c | Bursar Service | Process Command | Calculates application amount (min of stipend and balance), creates ledger entries |\n| 7d | Bursar Service | Publish Events | Emits `StipendAppliedToTuition` or `StipendApplicationToTuitionFailed` |\n| 8 | Saga Coordinator | Complete | Saga terminates successfully |\n\n### 1.3 Conditional Flow Logic\n\n```c\n// Pseudo-code for Step 7 decision logic\nif (query_auto_payment_status(student_id, &status) == QUERY_OK) {\n    if (status.opt_in_consent == true && status.tuition_balance_due > 0) {\n        int64_t apply_amount = MIN(stipend_amount, status.tuition_balance_due);\n        apply_stipend_to_tuition_command cmd = {\n            .student_id = student_id,\n            .stipend_disbursement_id = disbursement_id,\n            .amount_to_apply = apply_amount,\n            .currency = \"USD\"\n        };\n        bursar_service_execute(&cmd);\n    }\n    // If opt_in=false OR balance=0, skip auto-payment (no error)\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Saga Pattern Integration\n\n### 2.1 Design Decision: Extend Existing Saga\n\n**Recommendation:** Extend the existing `DisburseStipendSaga` in `/app/project/src/services/financial_aid_service/application/use_cases/disburse_stipend_saga.h` rather than creating a new saga.\n\n**Justification:**\n\n1. **Transactional Coherence**: Applying financial aid to tuition is a logical extension of the stipend disbursement process. The funds should be applied atomically as part of the same business transaction context.\n\n2. **Shared Correlation ID**: The existing saga's `correlation_id_t` can propagate through to the tuition application, maintaining a complete audit trail.\n\n3. **Unified Compensation**: If the tuition application step fails, compensation can leverage the existing rollback infrastructure (release_budget, reverse_transfer).\n\n4. **Simpler Mental Model**: A single saga for \"disburse stipend and optionally apply to tuition\" is easier to reason about than two separate sagas coordinating.\n\n5. **Existing Pattern Compatibility**: The current saga already follows the VTable callback pattern (see lines 147-155 of `disburse_stipend_saga.h`), making extension straightforward.\n\n### 2.2 Extended Saga Definition\n\n```c\n/* Extended callbacks for disburse_stipend_saga.h */\n\ntypedef struct {\n    bool     opt_in_consent;\n    int64_t  tuition_balance_due;\n    char     currency[4];\n} auto_payment_status_t;\n\n/* New callback: Query auto-payment eligibility */\ntypedef bool (*query_auto_payment_status_fn)(\n    uint64_t                   student_id,\n    auto_payment_status_t     *status_out,\n    saga_error_t              *err_out);\n\n/* New callback: Apply stipend to tuition */\ntypedef bool (*apply_stipend_to_tuition_fn)(\n    const stipend_disbursement_request_t *req,\n    int64_t                               amount_to_apply,\n    uint64_t                             *ledger_entry_id_out,\n    saga_error_t                         *err_out);\n\n/* New callback: Reverse tuition application (compensation) */\ntypedef bool (*reverse_tuition_application_fn)(\n    uint64_t       ledger_entry_id,\n    saga_error_t  *err_out);\n\n/* Extended VTable */\ntypedef struct {\n    /* Existing callbacks */\n    validate_eligibility_fn   validate_eligibility;\n    reserve_budget_fn         reserve_budget;\n    release_budget_fn         release_budget;\n    initiate_transfer_fn      initiate_transfer;\n    reverse_transfer_fn       reverse_transfer;\n    record_ledger_entry_fn    record_ledger_entry;\n    publish_domain_event_fn   publish_domain_event;\n\n    /* NEW: Auto-payment callbacks */\n    query_auto_payment_status_fn   query_auto_payment_status;\n    apply_stipend_to_tuition_fn    apply_stipend_to_tuition;\n    reverse_tuition_application_fn reverse_tuition_application;\n} stipend_disbursement_callbacks_t;\n```\n\n### 2.3 Extended Saga Steps\n\nThe modified saga flow adds a new **Step 6** (inserted between existing steps 5 and the completion):\n\n| Step | Name | Forward Action | Compensation |\n|------|------|----------------|--------------|\n| 1 | Validate Eligibility | `validate_eligibility()` | N/A (no state change) |\n| 2 | Reserve Budget | `reserve_budget()` | `release_budget()` |\n| 3 | Initiate Transfer | `initiate_transfer()` | `reverse_transfer()` |\n| 4 | Record Ledger Entry | `record_ledger_entry()` | Ledger reversal entry |\n| 5 | Publish Domain Event | `publish_domain_event(\"StipendDisbursed\")` | Record reversal event |\n| **6** | **Apply to Tuition (Conditional)** | `apply_stipend_to_tuition()` | `reverse_tuition_application()` |\n\n### 2.4 Compensation Chain\n\nIf **Step 6 fails** after the tuition application was partially or fully processed:\n\n```c\n/* Compensation sequence for Step 6 failure */\nbool compensation_step_6(saga_context_t *ctx) {\n    saga_error_t err = SAGA_ERROR_INIT();\n\n    // Step 6 compensation: Reverse tuition application\n    if (ctx->tuition_ledger_entry_id != 0) {\n        cb->reverse_tuition_application(ctx->tuition_ledger_entry_id, &err);\n    }\n\n    // Continue with existing compensation chain (steps 5 -> 4 -> 3 -> 2)\n    cb->record_ledger_entry(req, \"STIPEND_DISBURSEMENT_REVERSED\", ctx->network_tx_id, &err);\n    cb->reverse_transfer(ctx->network_tx_id, &err);\n    cb->release_budget(ctx->reservation_id, &err);\n\n    return true; // Best-effort compensation\n}\n```\n\n### 2.5 Idempotency Considerations\n\nPer the saga documentation at `/app/project/docs/architecture/04_saga_pattern.md` (lines 382-386):\n\n> \"Compensations are **idempotent**: every reverse handler can run twice safely, a mandatory property in at-least-once delivery environments.\"\n\nThe `reverse_tuition_application_fn` must:\n1. Accept a `ledger_entry_id` for precise targeting\n2. Check if reversal already exists before creating\n3. Use the same `correlation_id` for traceability\n\n---\n\n## 3. New Domain Events\n\n### 3.1 Event Definitions\n\nThree new domain events are required:\n\n#### 3.1.1 `StipendAppliedToTuition`\n\n**Publisher:** `bursar_service`\n**Consumers:** `projections_service`, `notifications_service` (future), audit trail\n\n```c\n/* events/stipend_applied_to_tuition.h */\n#ifndef EDUPAY_EVENT_STIPEND_APPLIED_TO_TUITION_H\n#define EDUPAY_EVENT_STIPEND_APPLIED_TO_TUITION_H\n\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\n#define STIPEND_APPLIED_EVENT_VERSION 1\n\ntypedef struct {\n    uint8_t  version;\n    char     event_id[37];              /* UUID v4 */\n    char     correlation_id[37];        /* Links to original StipendDisbursed */\n\n    /* Student identification */\n    uint64_t student_id;\n    char     student_account_id[32];\n\n    /* Transaction details */\n    uint64_t stipend_disbursement_id;   /* FK to StipendDisbursed event */\n    uint64_t ledger_entry_id;           /* Bursar ledger entry */\n\n    /* Monetary details */\n    int64_t  amount_applied_minor;      /* Amount in minor units (cents) */\n    int64_t  remaining_stipend_minor;   /* Leftover stipend not applied */\n    int64_t  remaining_balance_minor;   /* Outstanding tuition after application */\n    char     currency[4];               /* ISO-4217 */\n\n    /* Timestamps */\n    time_t   occurred_at_utc;\n    char     term[8];                   /* e.g., \"2024SP\" */\n} stipend_applied_to_tuition_event_t;\n\n#endif\n```\n\n#### 3.1.2 `StipendApplicationToTuitionFailed`\n\n**Publisher:** `bursar_service`\n**Consumers:** `projections_service`, `alerting_service`, audit trail\n\n```c\n/* events/stipend_application_failed.h */\n#ifndef EDUPAY_EVENT_STIPEND_APPLICATION_FAILED_H\n#define EDUPAY_EVENT_STIPEND_APPLICATION_FAILED_H\n\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\ntypedef enum {\n    APPLY_FAIL_ACCOUNT_SUSPENDED,\n    APPLY_FAIL_ACCOUNT_CLOSED,\n    APPLY_FAIL_CURRENCY_MISMATCH,\n    APPLY_FAIL_LEDGER_ERROR,\n    APPLY_FAIL_CONSENT_WITHDRAWN,\n    APPLY_FAIL_NO_BALANCE_DUE,\n    APPLY_FAIL_INTERNAL\n} stipend_application_failure_reason_t;\n\ntypedef struct {\n    uint8_t  version;\n    char     event_id[37];\n    char     correlation_id[37];\n\n    uint64_t student_id;\n    uint64_t stipend_disbursement_id;\n\n    stipend_application_failure_reason_t reason;\n    char     error_message[128];\n\n    time_t   occurred_at_utc;\n} stipend_application_failed_event_t;\n\n#endif\n```\n\n#### 3.1.3 `TuitionApplicationReversed`\n\n**Publisher:** `bursar_service` (during saga compensation)\n**Consumers:** `projections_service`, audit trail\n\n```c\n/* events/tuition_application_reversed.h */\n#ifndef EDUPAY_EVENT_TUITION_APPLICATION_REVERSED_H\n#define EDUPAY_EVENT_TUITION_APPLICATION_REVERSED_H\n\n#include <stdint.h>\n#include <time.h>\n\ntypedef struct {\n    uint8_t  version;\n    char     event_id[37];\n    char     correlation_id[37];\n\n    uint64_t student_id;\n    uint64_t original_ledger_entry_id;\n    uint64_t reversal_ledger_entry_id;\n\n    int64_t  amount_reversed_minor;\n    char     currency[4];\n\n    char     reason[128];              /* e.g., \"Saga compensation\" */\n    time_t   occurred_at_utc;\n} tuition_application_reversed_event_t;\n\n#endif\n```\n\n### 3.2 Event Flow Diagram\n\n```\n┌──────────────────────┐         ┌──────────────────────┐         ┌──────────────────────┐\n│  financial_aid_svc   │         │    bursar_service    │         │ projections_service  │\n└──────────┬───────────┘         └──────────┬───────────┘         └──────────┬───────────┘\n           │                                │                                │\n           │  StipendDisbursed              │                                │\n           │───────────────────────────────>│                                │\n           │                                │                                │\n           │ ApplyStipendToTuition (cmd)    │                                │\n           │───────────────────────────────>│                                │\n           │                                │                                │\n           │                                │ StipendAppliedToTuition        │\n           │                                │───────────────────────────────>│\n           │                                │                                │\n           │                                │                      ┌─────────┴─────────┐\n           │                                │                      │ Update:           │\n           │                                │                      │ - account_balance │\n           │                                │                      │ - tuition_due     │\n           │                                │                      │ - payment_history │\n           │                                │                      └─────────┬─────────┘\n           │                                │                                │\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. CQRS/Event Sourcing Impact\n\n### 4.1 Write Model (Event Store) Changes\n\n#### 4.1.1 New Event Types in Event Store\n\nThe following new event types must be registered with the event store interface defined in `/app/project/src/core/event_sourcing/event_store_interface.h`:\n\n```c\n/* Add to event type registry */\nstatic const char* EVENT_TYPES[] = {\n    /* Existing */\n    \"PaymentAuthorized\",\n    \"StipendDisbursed\",\n    /* ... */\n\n    /* New for Auto-Payment */\n    \"StipendAppliedToTuition\",\n    \"StipendApplicationToTuitionFailed\",\n    \"TuitionApplicationReversed\"\n};\n```\n\n#### 4.1.2 Affected Aggregates in Bursar Service\n\n**1. Account Aggregate** (`/app/project/src/services/bursar_service/domain/account.h`):\n\nThe `account_credit` and `account_debit` functions (lines 155-187) will be invoked during the auto-payment process. No modifications needed to the aggregate itself, but new event handlers must be wired:\n\n```c\n/* In bursar_service command handler */\nAccountResult result = account_debit(\n    student_account,    /* Source: student's stipend funds */\n    amount_to_apply,\n    currency,\n    trace_id\n);\n\nif (result == ACC_OK) {\n    // Credit tuition receivables account\n    result = account_credit(\n        tuition_account,\n        amount_to_apply,\n        currency,\n        trace_id\n    );\n}\n```\n\n**2. Ledger Aggregate** (`/app/project/src/services/bursar_service/domain/ledger.h`):\n\nNew ledger entries must be created using `ledger_entry_create()` (lines 196-212):\n\n```c\n/* Create ledger entry for tuition payment */\nledger_entry_t entry;\nledger_entry_create(\n    &entry,\n    student_id,\n    \"TUITION_RECEIVABLE\",      /* account_id */\n    LEDGER_ENTRY_CREDIT,       /* type: credit reduces receivable */\n    amount,\n    \"Auto-payment from stipend disbursement\",\n    &correlation_id            /* links to saga */\n);\n\nledger_post_entry(ledger, &entry);\n```\n\n### 4.2 Read Model (Projections) Changes\n\n#### 4.2.1 Dashboard Projector Updates\n\nThe dashboard projector at `/app/project/src/projections_service/event_handlers/dashboard_projector.h` must be extended to handle the new events.\n\n**Current Event Types (lines 45-53):**\n```c\ntypedef enum {\n    EVT_UNDEFINED = 0,\n    EVT_PAYMENT_PROCESSED,\n    EVT_TRANSACTION_SETTLED,\n    EVT_FRAUD_FLAGGED,\n    EVT_CURRENCY_CONVERTED,\n    EVT_SCHOLARSHIP_DISBURSED\n} ledger_event_type_t;\n```\n\n**Extended Event Types:**\n```c\ntypedef enum {\n    EVT_UNDEFINED = 0,\n    EVT_PAYMENT_PROCESSED,\n    EVT_TRANSACTION_SETTLED,\n    EVT_FRAUD_FLAGGED,\n    EVT_CURRENCY_CONVERTED,\n    EVT_SCHOLARSHIP_DISBURSED,\n    /* NEW */\n    EVT_STIPEND_APPLIED_TO_TUITION,\n    EVT_STIPEND_APPLICATION_FAILED,\n    EVT_TUITION_APPLICATION_REVERSED\n} ledger_event_type_t;\n```\n\n**New Fold Function:**\n```c\n/* Add to dashboard_projector.h */\n\nstatic inline bool _fold_stipend_applied_to_tuition(sqlite3 *db,\n                                                     const char *payload)\n{\n    double amount = 0.0;\n    double remaining_balance = 0.0;\n\n    if (!_json_extract_double(payload, \"\\\"amount_applied_minor\\\"\", &amount))\n        return false;\n\n    _json_extract_double(payload, \"\\\"remaining_balance_minor\\\"\", &remaining_balance);\n\n    sqlite3_stmt *stmt = NULL;\n    bool ok = false;\n\n    /* Update student's tuition dashboard */\n    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(\n            db,\n            \"UPDATE student_tuition_summary \"\n            \"SET tuition_paid = tuition_paid + ?, \"\n            \"    balance_due = ?, \"\n            \"    last_payment_date = datetime('now'), \"\n            \"    auto_payment_count = auto_payment_count + 1 \"\n            \"WHERE student_id = ?;\",\n            -1, &stmt, NULL) == SQLITE_OK)\n    {\n        sqlite3_bind_double(stmt, 1, amount / 100.0);  /* Convert cents to dollars */\n        sqlite3_bind_double(stmt, 2, remaining_balance / 100.0);\n        /* student_id binding would come from payload extraction */\n        ok = (sqlite3_step(stmt) == SQLITE_DONE);\n    }\n\n    sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n    return ok;\n}\n```\n\n**Update Event Handler Switch (around line 383):**\n```c\nswitch (evt->type) {\n    /* ... existing cases ... */\n\n    case EVT_STIPEND_APPLIED_TO_TUITION:\n        if (_json_extract_double(evt->payload, \"\\\"amount_applied_minor\\\"\", &amount))\n            fold_ok = _fold_stipend_applied_to_tuition(prj->db, evt->payload);\n        break;\n\n    case EVT_TUITION_APPLICATION_REVERSED:\n        /* Reverse the previous application */\n        if (_json_extract_double(evt->payload, \"\\\"amount_reversed_minor\\\"\", &amount))\n            fold_ok = _fold_tuition_application_reversed(prj->db, evt->payload);\n        break;\n\n    default:\n        fold_ok = true;\n        break;\n}\n```\n\n#### 4.2.2 New Read Model Tables\n\n```sql\n-- Auto-Payment Consent Tracking (for query in saga step 7a)\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS student_auto_payment_preferences (\n    student_id       TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n    opt_in_consent   BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,\n    consent_date     DATETIME,\n    consent_version  INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n    updated_at       DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n);\n\n-- Student Tuition Summary (materialized view for dashboard)\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS student_tuition_summary (\n    student_id           TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n    tuition_charged      REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0,\n    tuition_paid         REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0,\n    balance_due          REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.0,\n    last_payment_date    DATETIME,\n    auto_payment_count   INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    updated_at           DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP\n);\n\n-- Auto-Payment History (for audit and student portal)\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS auto_payment_history (\n    id                      INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n    student_id              TEXT NOT NULL,\n    stipend_disbursement_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n    amount_applied          REAL NOT NULL,\n    remaining_stipend       REAL NOT NULL,\n    remaining_balance       REAL NOT NULL,\n    currency                TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'USD',\n    occurred_at             DATETIME NOT NULL,\n    created_at              DATETIME DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,\n\n    FOREIGN KEY (student_id) REFERENCES student_auto_payment_preferences(student_id)\n);\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Compliance and Consent\n\n### 5.1 Regulatory Framework\n\nAs documented in `/app/project/docs/architecture/05_security_and_compliance.md` and `/app/project/docs/lessons/L03_Regulatory_Compliance_in_Code.md`:\n\n| Regulation | Requirement | Implementation |\n|------------|-------------|----------------|\n| **FERPA** | Consent required for financial record access/modification | Explicit opt-in flag with versioned consent |\n| **PCI-DSS** | Secure handling of payment data | No PAN/CVV stored; use tokenized references |\n| **SOC 2** | Audit trail for all financial operations | Event sourcing provides immutable audit |\n\n### 5.2 Consent Storage and Management\n\n#### 5.2.1 Consent Data Model\n\nThe consent flag will be stored in a dedicated table maintained by the `admissions_service` (owner of Student aggregate) and projected to the read model:\n\n```c\n/* Domain: Student Preferences Aggregate (admissions_service) */\ntypedef struct {\n    uint64_t student_id;\n    bool     tuition_auto_pay_enabled;\n    time_t   consent_granted_at;\n    uint32_t consent_version;          /* For FERPA audit requirements */\n    char     consent_ip_address[46];   /* IPv6 max length */\n    char     consent_method[32];       /* \"web_portal\", \"mobile_app\", etc. */\n} student_auto_payment_consent_t;\n```\n\n#### 5.2.2 Consent Events\n\n```c\n/* Event: AutoPaymentConsentGranted */\ntypedef struct {\n    uint8_t  version;\n    char     event_id[37];\n    uint64_t student_id;\n    bool     consent_granted;\n    uint32_t consent_version;\n    char     consent_method[32];\n    char     ip_address[46];\n    time_t   occurred_at_utc;\n} auto_payment_consent_granted_event_t;\n\n/* Event: AutoPaymentConsentRevoked */\ntypedef struct {\n    uint8_t  version;\n    char     event_id[37];\n    uint64_t student_id;\n    uint32_t previous_consent_version;\n    char     revocation_reason[128];\n    time_t   occurred_at_utc;\n} auto_payment_consent_revoked_event_t;\n```\n\n### 5.3 Consent Check in Workflow\n\nThe consent check occurs in **Step 7a** of the extended saga:\n\n```c\n/* Implementation of query_auto_payment_status callback */\nbool impl_query_auto_payment_status(\n    uint64_t                student_id,\n    auto_payment_status_t  *status_out,\n    saga_error_t           *err_out)\n{\n    /* Query the read model (projections_service) */\n    sqlite3_stmt *stmt = NULL;\n    bool found = false;\n\n    const char *sql =\n        \"SELECT p.opt_in_consent, t.balance_due, t.currency \"\n        \"FROM student_auto_payment_preferences p \"\n        \"LEFT JOIN student_tuition_summary t ON p.student_id = t.student_id \"\n        \"WHERE p.student_id = ?;\";\n\n    if (sqlite3_prepare_v2(read_model_db, sql, -1, &stmt, NULL) == SQLITE_OK) {\n        sqlite3_bind_int64(stmt, 1, (sqlite3_int64)student_id);\n\n        if (sqlite3_step(stmt) == SQLITE_ROW) {\n            status_out->opt_in_consent = sqlite3_column_int(stmt, 0) != 0;\n            status_out->tuition_balance_due = sqlite3_column_int64(stmt, 1);\n            strncpy(status_out->currency,\n                    (const char*)sqlite3_column_text(stmt, 2),\n                    sizeof(status_out->currency) - 1);\n            found = true;\n        }\n        sqlite3_finalize(stmt);\n    }\n\n    if (!found) {\n        /* No consent record = no opt-in (FERPA compliant default) */\n        status_out->opt_in_consent = false;\n        status_out->tuition_balance_due = 0;\n    }\n\n    return true;  /* Query succeeded (even if no data found) */\n}\n```\n\n### 5.4 FERPA Compliance Safeguards\n\nPer the compliance guard implementation in `/app/project/docs/lessons/L03_Regulatory_Compliance_in_Code.md`:\n\n1. **Explicit Consent Only**: Default is `opt_in_consent = false`\n2. **Consent Versioning**: Each consent update increments version number\n3. **Audit Trail**: All consent changes are event-sourced\n4. **Immediate Revocation**: Consent check happens at execution time, not at enrollment time\n5. **Description Scrubbing**: Use `ledger_ferpa_scrub()` (line 321 of `ledger.h`) on any student-identifiable information in ledger descriptions\n\n---\n\n## 6. when consent is granted and balance exists */\nvoid test_auto_payment_happy_path(void) {\n    setup_student_with_consent(STUDENT_ID, true);\n    setup_tuition_balance(STUDENT_ID, 500000);  // $5000.00\n\n    stipend_disbursement_request_t req = {\n        .student_id = STUDENT_ID,\n        .stipend_cents = 200000,  // $2000.00\n        .currency = \"USD\"\n    };\n\n    saga_error_t err;\n    bool result = disburse_stipend_run(&req, &test_callbacks, &err);\n\n    assert(result == true);\n    assert(err.code == SAGA_OK);\n    assert(get_tuition_balance(STUDENT_ID) == 300000);  // $3000.00 remaining\n    assert(get_auto_payment_count(STUDENT_ID) == 1);\n}\n\n/* Test: Saga skips auto-payment when consent is not granted */\nvoid test_auto_payment_no_consent(void) {\n    setup_student_with_consent(STUDENT_ID, false);  // No consent\n    setup_tuition_balance(STUDENT_ID, 500000);\n\n    stipend_disbursement_request_t req = { /* ... */ };\n\n    saga_error_t err;\n    bool result = disburse_stipend_run(&req, &test_callbacks, &err);\n\n    assert(result == true);  // Saga succeeds\n    assert(get_tuition_balance(STUDENT_ID) == 500000);  // Unchanged\n    assert(get_auto_payment_count(STUDENT_ID) == 0);\n}\n\n/* Test: Compensation reverses auto-payment on later failure */\nvoid test_auto_payment_compensation(void) {\n    setup_student_with_consent(STUDENT_ID, true);\n    setup_tuition_balance(STUDENT_ID, 500000);\n\n    test_callbacks.publish_domain_event = failing_publish;  // Inject failure\n\n    saga_error_t err;\n    bool result = disburse_stipend_run(&req, &test_callbacks, &err);\n\n    assert(result == false);\n    assert(err.code == SAGA_ERR_EVENT_BUS);\n    assert(get_tuition_balance(STUDENT_ID) == 500000);  // Restored\n    assert(get_reversal_count(STUDENT_ID) == 1);  // Compensation ran\n}\n```\n\n### 7.3 Monitoring and Observability\n\nAdd OpenTelemetry spans (per production hardening student_id,\n    int64_t     amount,\n    bool        success)\n{\n    char trace_msg[256];\n    snprintf(trace_msg, sizeof(trace_msg),\n             \"[SAGA:AutoPayment] step=%s student=%\" PRIu64\n
